task: Retrofitted Dubai UAE city file under v2026-05-27 schema (platform-aware bucketing, sub-cluster expansion, OUT-OF-SCOPE → Quanta-LR wedge re-tags). Seeded three empty MENA reference files (out-of-range-marquee-routes, grid-quality-by-market, mena-blackout-calendar).
learnings:
  - methodology: **Single-anchor city with 5+ cross-border Quanta-LR redirect rows** — Dubai is the first retrofitted city where the sub-cluster table picks up *five* OUT-OF-SCOPE→Quanta-LR-wedge rows (Sir Bani Yas, Khasab, Muscat, Doha, Manama). Pattern likely recurs at any well-positioned hub-emirate/hub-city in a multi-jurisdiction sea basin: Dubai → GCC, Singapore → Riau+Johor+Batam-Bintan, Miami → Bahamas+Caribbean, HK → Pearl River Delta cities. Worth explicit schema callout: "**regional-hub-with-cross-border-Quanta-spokes**" as a sub-cluster taxonomy pattern.
  - methodology: **Edge-of-Pioneer single-jurisdiction routes flip cleanly to Quanta-LR routine** — DXB↔AUH at ~65–75 nm was the canonical "edge with thin reserve" example; Quanta-LR doesn't just make it possible, it makes the *timetable* viable (no Ghantoot/Yas mid-route top-up, no weather-margin gymnastics). The qualitative jump from "feasible via top-up" to "routine direct" is the Series-B narrative lever, not just range arithmetic. Worth surfacing as a distinct framing in N30 implications: "Quanta-LR upgrades edge-of-Pioneer routes from feasibility-with-gymnastics to routine-timetable."
  - methodology: **Bahrain sandbox jurisdiction as deliberate Quanta-LR launch-market wedge** — Bahrain's regulatory-sandbox posture (single national regulator, fast decisions, prior fintech sandbox track record) maps cleanly to "fastest GCC first-of-class certification path." Worth a standing prompt: when a city file has cross-border reach into a sandbox jurisdiction (Bahrain, Singapore, Estonia, Rwanda), explicitly frame that endpoint as a regulatory-velocity wedge regardless of underlying demand.
  - source-discovered: **No T8-equivalent statutory weather-trigger blackout in the Gulf** — confirmed Dubai/UAE NCM and Coast Guard issue advisories only, no mandatory port-closure regime parallel to HK Marine Dept T8. Material because the HK retrofit established statutory-weather-blackout as a class; Gulf is the *absence-case* and that's a positive for pilot reliability narrative.
  - source-discovered: **Royal-overlook constraint at AUH Corniche** (Qasr Al Watan + royal compounds along corniche) + **Sir Bani Yas as Sheikh Zayed legacy island** with Anantara overlay direct royal interest. Adds two MENA datapoints to the Doha West Bay / Jeddah Corniche / Bangkok Grand Palace catalogue. Schema-level royal-overlook prompt now confirmed across 6+ markets.
  - source-discovered: **No documented hydrofoil/jetfoil graveyard in GCC cross-border corridors** — closest analog is Sharjah↔Bandar Lengeh / Dubai↔Bandar Abbas Iran-side, not relevant. Quanta-LR DXB↔Doha/Bahrain is genuinely greenfield premium-fast-ferry territory. Useful absence-case for the historical-jetfoil-graveyards.md reference.
  - source-discovered: **Dubai abras (RTA-managed cooperative, ~15M trips/yr)** confirmed as protected indigenous water-craft class — pairs with Thai longtails, Mumbai koli, Bali jukung, Venice gondolieri, Goa batel, Philippine bangka. Creek-side framing must be premium/private tier.
  - schema-gap: **Sub-cluster taxonomy missing "regional-hub-with-cross-border-Quanta-spokes" pattern** — emerges naturally from Dubai but not currently prompted. Likely applies to Singapore, Miami, HK, Istanbul. Worth surfacing in CITY_TEMPLATE.md sub-clusters conventions.
  - schema-gap: **No prompt for "edge-of-Pioneer → routine-timetable on Quanta-LR" qualitative upgrade framing** — currently N30 implications prompts treat the platform fork as a binary range check; the operational-timetable upgrade story (no top-up, no weather-margin gymnastics) is more powerful and not currently surfaced.
  - schema-gap: **"Sandbox-jurisdiction endpoint" prompt** — when a cross-border Quanta-LR wedge reaches a regulatory-sandbox jurisdiction (Bahrain, Singapore, Estonia, Rwanda), frame as regulatory-velocity wedge. Currently buried in cultural-protocol bullet's sandbox-attributes line.
  - efficiency: MENA reference files now seeded (out-of-range-marquee-routes, grid-quality-by-market, mena-blackout-calendar). Subsequent MENA retrofits (Abu Dhabi, Doha, Bahrain, Muscat, Jeddah, NEOM, RSG, AMAALA) should cite rather than re-derive. Back-fill priority: AUH first, since it shares 4 of the 5 Quanta-LR cross-border spokes.

date: 2026-05-27
subagent: navier-city-retrofit.md
task: Retrofitted Abu Dhabi UAE city file under v2026-05-27 schema. Extended out-of-range-marquee-routes + mena-blackout-calendar reference files.
learnings:
  - methodology: **Sister-city retrofits should be done in pairs (Dubai → AUH, future Doha → Bahrain, NEOM → AMAALA)** — when sub-cluster tables share 4+ cross-border Quanta-LR spokes, doing the second file <1 hour after the first reuses 80% of the route table with mirror-image distance/jurisdiction data. Material efficiency gain; flag for parent agent when sequencing future retrofits.
  - methodology: **Headline OUT-OF-SCOPE→Quanta-LR flip is *intra-emirate* not cross-border** — AUH↔Sir Bani Yas (~135 nm) is the single most operationally clarifying flip in the file because (a) it's intra-UAE so no cross-border friction, (b) Pioneer II had no viable forced-two-tier mid-route node (Mugheirah/Marawah unbuilt), so Pioneer II architecture was genuinely broken; (c) Anantara + Sheikh Zayed legacy royal overlay means the wedge is also the most narratively powerful. Likely analog: Dubai↔Hatta if waterway existed, KSA↔intra-Red-Sea between giga-projects, Bali↔Sumba (NIHI), Bali↔Komodo, Phuket↔Surin. Worth tagging in schema as the "intra-jurisdiction, no-mid-route-node, royal-overlay Quanta-LR flip" archetype — the highest-value single-route flip class.
  - methodology: **Dual-convener royal channel** — AUH royal channel likely distinct from Dubai-side Sheikha Maktoum. Pattern likely recurs in any federal/multi-emirate or multi-royal-house jurisdiction: KSA (different princes own different giga-projects — NEOM vs. RSG vs. AMAALA vs. Diriyah), Malaysia (federal vs. state sultans), pre-unified Italy historical analog. Schema implication: "convener" column in Holds & sensitivities should be per-sub-cluster when royal/sovereign-adjacent geography spans multiple houses or chambers.
  - methodology: **F1 + National Day + Commemoration Day cluster into a single combined 1-week early-Dec window in AUH** — first time three distinct blackout classes (marquee marine event, national-day PR, day-of-mourning) overlap in one week. Suggests schema treatment: when 3+ blackouts compound within 7 days, treat as a single combined window rather than enumerating separately. Likely analog: Bahrain F1 + Mar founding period; Qatar F1 (Nov) + Dec 18 National Day.
  - source-discovered: **AUH↔Sir Bani Yas Pioneer II architecture is genuinely broken pre-Quanta-LR** because no mid-route charging node exists between Yas (NE side) and Sir Bani Yas (~135 nm W) — Mugheirah/Marawah/Sila are AD Ports marinas but greenfield-pedestal, not built. So this is a *clean* OUT-OF-SCOPE→Quanta-LR flip without forced-two-tier fallback. Distinct from BKK↔Pattaya (Pattaya is built) or AUH↔Dubai (Ghantoot/Yas exist as top-up). Useful "no-fallback Pioneer II" archetype for Series-B narrative.

Adds seaplane-incumbent classification to AUH for the Sir Bani Yas wedge specifically (not the full city) — partial overlap with Maldives/NEOM/BVI seaplane-incumbent positioning. Standing wedge framing for Sir Bani Yas: **night-capable + all-weather + brand-controlled guest arrival**, not "faster than Rotana Jet."
  - source-discovered: AUH↔DXB has a documented public-ferry graveyard of sorts — repeated RTA/ITC ferry proposals 2015–2020s never landed commercially. Quanta-LR fixes timetable reliability specifically — different graveyard-resurrection lever than other corridors. Adds to historical-jetfoil-graveyards.md back-fill when that ref is created.
  - source-discovered: **No major abra/water-taxi cooperative in AUH** — unlike Dubai Creek's 15M-pax/yr RTA-managed abra fleet, AUH has minimal indigenous water-craft cooperative footprint. Operationally significant: the protected-cooperative constraint that gates Creek-side framing in Dubai is *absent* in AUH. Premium-product greenfield without the political-displacement risk.

Schema convention should clarify: when sister cities share spokes, the spokes should appear in both sub-cluster tables but the city files should cross-reference rather than duplicate detailed wedge analysis. Worth a schema addition.

learnings:
  - methodology: **Pioneer II routine cross-border is a NEW distinct route class** — Manama↔Dammam (~30 nm) is the first documented "Pioneer II routine + cross-jurisdiction" wedge across all retrofits. Distinct from edge-of-Pioneer-single-jurisdiction (BKK↔Pattaya, Jeddah↔KAEC) AND from edge-of-Pioneer-cross-jurisdiction (HK↔Macau ~37 nm one-way). Manama↔Dammam at 30 nm is comfortably inside Pioneer II with margin AND cross-border — the **shortest premium cross-border maritime leg in MENA**. Worth distinguishing as a fourth Pioneer II range sub-class: `Pioneer II routine cross-border` (causeway-bypass premium, ferry-bypass-of-bridge archetype). Likely analogs: Miami↔Bimini (~50 nm — at Pioneer edge), Singapore↔Batam (~25 nm), future Cuba↔Florida (politically gated).
  - methodology: **Sister-pair routing table as a structural artifact** — when two retrofitted city files share 3+ cross-border Quanta-LR spokes (Dubai+AUH was the first pair; Doha+Manama is the second), an explicit sister-pair routing table inside BOTH files (mirrored) is the cleanest way to enforce naming/distance consistency. Worth promoting to schema as a sub-clusters convention for sister-city / regional-mesh files. Likely future sister-pairs: NEOM+AMAALA+RSG (Red Sea triple), Riyadh+Dammam (KSA internal), Singapore+Batam+Bintan (Riau mesh), Miami+Bahamas+Bimini (Caribbean), HK+Macau (Pearl River).
  - methodology: **Royal-channel convener status as a structured open-question** — Doha file's single highest-priority open question is "does Sampriti have a Qatar royal channel?" because absence gates ~half the wedges. Worth promoting to a structured schema field per file (or per sub-cluster in multi-house jurisdictions): **"Royal/sovereign convener: [identified / unknown — research priority / not applicable]."** Currently buried in Holds free text. The convener field complements the convener-per-sub-cluster prompt added in the AUH retrofit (DXB-side Sheikha Maktoum vs. AUH-side TBD).
  - methodology: **"Causeway-bypass premium" as a recurring wedge archetype** — Manama↔Dammam's narrative anchor is the King Fahd Causeway congestion. This is the same archetypal positioning as future Singapore↔Johor (Causeway / Second Link bypass), Miami↔Key West / Bahamas (bridge bypass), HCMC↔Phu Quoc — congested-fixed-link-bypass-by-water. Worth a standing wedge framing: "**bypass-of-incumbent-fixed-link**" — pairs with the gateway-city reframe but inverts it (boat *replaces* a congested fixed link, vs. boat *connects to* an air gateway).
  - methodology: **Cultural-protocol GRADIENT across nominally permissive markets** — Doha and Manama are both legal-zoned-permissive, but Bahrain extends permissiveness to public-facing water operations whereas Doha confines it to hotel-anchored / per-resort enforcement (Banana Island enforces resort house rules stricter than Pearl/Lusail). Worth refining the "legal zoned exemption" sub-prompt to capture this gradient: **fully permissive public (Bahrain) → permissive hotel-anchored (Qatar / UAE Dubai/AUH inside-licensed-property) → conservative public, permissive zoned (KSA RSG/AMAALA tourism zones).** Three-tier gradient, not binary.
  - source-discovered: **First-of-class certification regimes reference file seeded** — Bahrain MTT is canonical fastest-GCC entry path; Singapore MPA is the SEA equivalent. Pioneer II cert can be pursued NOW; Quanta-LR cert can only start post-sea-trial data (mid-2026). This is the most operationally clarifying piece of the Bahrain wedge: the cert itself is the wedge, not the boat. Pair with Jaideep-direct MTT for the cleanest unblocked move in the entire Navier MENA map.
  - source-discovered: **Hawar Islands KSA-disputed-historically-now-resolved (ICJ 2001)** — sovereignty no longer an issue but environmental sensitivity remains. Stay tonally careful; KSA-side guests touring Hawar should be checked for any residual sensitivities. Niche but worth flagging in any KSA Eastern Province cross-border pilot.
  - source-discovered: **Qatar Free Zones Authority (QFZA) as possible Doha-side sandbox host** — parallel to Bahrain MTT but Doha-side. Has not been pursued as a Navier door but warrants explicit research. If real, partially bypasses the Qatar royal-channel hold for a Doha pilot via sandbox framing.
  - source-discovered: **Manama↔Dubai ~265 nm is the longest single-leg in the GCC Quanta-LR mesh** — well within Quanta-LR 2000 nm spec but at the upper end of routine cruise distances; route-planning discipline / fuel-headroom flagging matters more than at 110-225 nm legs. Worth standardising a "**upper-Quanta cruise**" caveat tag for any single-leg >250 nm to flag fuel/weather/route-planning headroom requirements.
  - source-discovered: **Bahrain Diyar Al Muharraq + Qatar Qetaifan Island are the GCC pair of open greenfield charging-design-in windows** (alongside Bahrain Marina BMDC). High-leverage Pioneer II infra wedges where design-in beats retrofit by orders of magnitude.
  - schema-gap: **Pioneer II routine cross-border** is a new range sub-class not currently named in the Sub-clusters Pioneer II bucketing convention. Currently the schema enumerates `in-range / edge-of-Pioneer / Pioneer-out` AND separately splits edge-of-Pioneer into single-jurisdiction vs. cross-jurisdiction. But `in-range cross-jurisdiction` (Manama↔Dammam) and `edge-of-Pioneer cross-jurisdiction` (HK↔Macau, future Singapore↔Batam) deserve symmetric naming. Worth a small schema clarification.
  - schema-gap: **Royal-channel convener status field** should be a structured schema field per file (top of Holds & sensitivities or top of Open Questions), not buried in free text. Especially for multi-house jurisdictions (UAE per-emirate, KSA per-prince, Malaysia per-state).

  - efficiency: **Doing Doha + Manama back-to-back in one batch (sister-pair payload approach)** reused ~70% of the cross-border route work — confirms the AUH-after-Dubai sister-pair efficiency learning. Recommend continued sister-pair batching: NEOM+AMAALA+RSG (Red Sea triple) next.

## 2026-05-27 — KSA Red Sea triple retrofit (red-sea-global-ksa.md + jeddah-ksa.md + neom-sindalah-ksa.md)

**Routes flipped from OUT-OF-SCOPE → Quanta-LR wedge (headline output):**
- **RSG ↔ AMAALA ~80 nm** (intra-RSG portfolio; canonical **greenfield-microgrid Quanta-LR diesel-refuel positioning lever globally** — both endpoints microgrid by design, diesel refuel collapses 2–5yr pedestal-civil-works dependency entirely)
- **Jeddah ↔ RSG (Shura) ~210 nm** — canonical gateway-city Quanta-LR line-haul instance globally
- **Jeddah ↔ AMAALA ~290 nm** — upper-Quanta cruise (Ocean Race 2027 VIP arrival pipeline)
- **Jeddah ↔ NEOM ~325 nm** — upper-Quanta cruise cross-giga-project
- **Jeddah ↔ Yanbu ~150 nm** — intra-KSA Red Sea coast
- **NEOM ↔ AMAALA ~200 nm** — cross-giga-project (different conveners)
- **NEOM ↔ RSG ~280–300 nm** — cross-giga-project upper-cruise

**New constraint-class documented (NEW schema vocabulary):**
- **"Geography-fit + politically-gated"** route class — NEOM↔Eilat ~30 nm fits Pioneer II routine cross-border archetype on range geometry but is operationally EXCLUDED under KSA flag-state policy. Distinct from range gate / security gate / cultural gate. Recommend adding to v2026-06+ schema as named sub-class.
- **Pioneer II routine cross-border instances #2 + #3 documented globally:** NEOM↔Aqaba (JO, #2) + NEOM↔Sharm El Sheikh (EG, #3), after Manama↔Dammam (#1). Catalogue now extends to NEOM cross-border triangle (4-corner geopolitical pattern: KSA/JO/EG/IL).
- **Flag-and-exclude security route class** first MENA instance: Jeddah↔Port Sudan ~165 nm (Sudan civil conflict post-2023). Distance within Quanta-LR spec; flagged-and-excluded.
- **HARD region-wide operational blackout sub-class** (Hajj) first documented MENA instance, distinct from national-day PR + weather-trigger + organizational-instability sub-classes.
- **"Cross-giga-project mesh" archetype** new this retrofit — multiple flagship developments by same sovereign (PIF) but different conveners (RSG-corporate Pagano / AMAALA MD line / NEOM CEO line). Coordinate narrative but do NOT bundle operationally.
- **Greenfield-microgrid Quanta-LR positioning lever** — RSG file is now the canonical instance globally; recommend schema explicitly call this out as a named lever in CITY_TEMPLATE.md alongside gateway-city reframe + edge-of-Pioneer routine-timetable upgrade.

**Reference-file gaps filled / still open:**
- `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` — added KSA Red Sea triple section + Pioneer II routine cross-border catalogue subsection (new).
- `mena-blackout-calendar.md` — added Hajj as HARD region-wide operational blackout (first instance); KSA national days; cross-border cascades for NEOM (JO May 25, EG Jul 23); NEOM org-instability blackout sub-class.
- `grid-quality-by-market.md` — added KSA cities + giga-project zones (grade C→A microgrid transition).
- `first-of-class-certification-regimes.md` — KSA giga-project SEZ regimes mentioned in city files but not yet seeded in reference file; **STILL OPEN** for next retrofit pass.
- `in-archipelago-mro-clusters.md` — RSG Turtle Bay + Oxagon flagged as Red Sea coast MRO design-in candidates; **reference file not yet updated** (gap).

**Schema sub-prompts that worked well / could improve:**
- v2026-05-27 platform-aware bucketing + three-tier cultural gradient + gateway-city reframe + Pioneer II routine cross-border sub-class all worked cleanly for KSA triple.
- **Missing/unclear:** schema doesn't yet have an explicit named class for "geography-fit + politically-gated" — currently shoehorned into Holds + Signals log. Recommend named sub-class.
- **Missing/unclear:** "convener identity research question" was implicit — NEOM has CEO churn → no stable named convener → flagged extensively in NEOM file. Recommend schema add an explicit `### Convener identity status` line under Holds (with values: stable / churn / unknown / royal-only) so files surface this consistently.
- **Missing/unclear:** sister-pair routing table convention was extended to **sister-trio** for this batch — recommend schema explicitly support N-tuple (sister-pair / sister-trio / sister-quad) routing tables and standardize cross-file mirroring rules.

**Unpromoted-entry watermark:** unpromoted entries now ~5 post-batch (still well under 10 threshold).

## 2026-05-27 — UAE Northern Emirates batch born-current build (RAK / Fujairah / Sharjah)

**Type:** methodology + schema-gap + source-discovered (multi)

**Files written:**
- `regions/mena/ras-al-khaimah-uae.md` (P1, candidate P0; Wynn Al Marjan 2027 anchor)
- `regions/mena/fujairah-uae.md` (P1; east-coast Gulf of Oman, no Hormuz)
- `regions/mena/sharjah-uae.md` (P2/Watch; cultural-heritage)
- `world-map/INDEX.md` updated MENA section.

### Source-discovered findings worth promoting

1. **Five UAE royal houses now mapped, not one.** Dubai (Al Maktoum) / Abu Dhabi (Al Nahyan) / RAK (Al Qasimi-RAK, Sheikh Saud bin Saqr) / Fujairah (Al Sharqi, Sheikh Hamad bin Mohammed) / Sharjah (Al Qasimi-Sharjah, Sheikh Sultan bin Muhammad). Sheikha Maktoum convener channel covers Dubai-side only with high confidence — extension to other emirates is the highest-priority open research item across all three files. Each emirate likely requires a separate convener intro.
2. **Pioneer II routine cross-border catalogue extended to 5 documented globally.** Added: **#4 RAK↔Khasab ~45 nm** (first UAE-side; first into Oman) and **#5 Dibba Fujairah↔Khasab ~40 nm** (first Gulf-of-Oman east-coast instance; no Hormuz TSS gate). Updated cross-region reference recommendation: append both to `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` Pioneer II routine cross-border catalogue.
3. **First three-jurisdiction same-bay constraint documented globally** — Dibba Bay (UAE Fujairah + UAE Sharjah + Oman Musandam). Proposed new schema vocabulary: "**multi-jurisdiction same-water-body**" as a sub-cluster constraint class.
4. **First intra-emirate three-tier cultural gradient documented globally** — Sharjah mainland (conservative-public) / licensed zoned exemption (Al Majaz F&B/event) / east-coast enclaves Khorfakkan-Kalba (permissive-zoned). Proposed schema action: promote "**intra-jurisdiction cultural gradient**" as a standing schema sub-prompt; consider adding "Cultural-protocol tier" column to Sub-clusters table for any market with intra-jurisdiction cultural variation (Sharjah file already does this experimentally).
5. **First UAE time-bound integrated-resort opening as charging-design-in window** — Wynn Al Marjan Island late-2026/early-2027 is functionally equivalent to Ocean Race 2027 / RSG-AMAALA microgrid commissioning / Sanur Terminal Bali / Hulhumalé Phase II Maldives as a pre-opening exclusive design-in window.
6. **Shortest UAE↔Muscat Quanta-LR route is Fujairah ↔ Muscat ~190 nm direct** (no Hormuz round-routing) — structural advantage Fujairah holds over DXB/AUH/RAK; should be flagged in `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` Quanta-LR catalogue.

### Schema-gap signals

- **Schema-gap A:** "Cultural-protocol tier" as Sub-clusters column for intra-jurisdiction-gradient markets (Sharjah pilot). Recommend promoting to standing schema column conditional on market type.
- **Schema-gap B:** "Multi-jurisdiction same-water-body" constraint class vocabulary missing from CITY_TEMPLATE. Recommend adding as a sub-cluster type or operating-constraint sub-section.
- **Schema-gap C:** Royal-house convener mapping at country level — UAE now has 5 distinct ruling houses; suggest a small reference file `/agent/home/navier/reference/uae-royal-houses-convener-map.md` to consolidate, including which Sampriti channels cover which.

### Methodology — born-current vs. retrofit

- **Born-current is materially faster than retrofit** (~30–40% time saving observed in this batch vs. prior UAE retrofits). Working from CITY_TEMPLATE.md v2026-05-27 + reading 2 sibling retrofit files (dubai/abu-dhabi) gave a strong scaffolding pattern.
- **Born-current still surfaces novel schema gaps** that a v2026-05-27-only template doesn't anticipate (intra-emirate cultural gradient, multi-jurisdiction same-bay). Born-current ≠ schema-complete; schema-gap-discovery rate is similar to retrofit, just routed at lower context cost.
- **Cross-emirate UAE coordination cost:** With 5 royal houses, the federation-wide UAE narrative becomes structurally harder to manage than the single-country sandbox markets (Bahrain Al Khalifa single-house, Qatar Al Thani single-house). Recommend the parent agent flag UAE as a **5-convener-channel program** in any Series-B / GTM-framing artifact rather than a single-country pilot.

### Efficiency

- Reading both sibling retrofit files dubai/abu-dhabi paid off — sub-clusters table conventions, holds-section structure, route-bucket vocabulary were directly reusable.
- Batch builds: writing all 3 files in sequence (RAK first as highest-value anchor, Fujairah second to bridge east-coast, Sharjah last as wrinkle to call out gradient) was efficient.

### Recommended parent actions

1. **Append RAK↔Khasab (#4) and Dibba↔Khasab (#5) Pioneer II routine cross-border entries to `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md`.**
2. **Append Fujairah↔Muscat ~190 nm direct to `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` Quanta-LR catalogue** (as shortest UAE↔Muscat option).
3. **Consider creating `/agent/home/navier/reference/uae-royal-houses-convener-map.md`** consolidating the 5 royal houses + convener channel mapping + Sheikha Maktoum portfolio scope confirmation.
4. **Sampriti research priority:** scope Sheikha Maktoum's portfolio across all 5 emirates; identify alternate convener channels (esp. Crown Prince Sheikh Mohammed bin Saud RAK; Crown Prince Sheikh Mohammed bin Hamad Fujairah; Sheikha Bodour bint Sultan Sharjah).
5. **Schema-gap consideration** for CITY_TEMPLATE next version: cultural-protocol tier column + multi-jurisdiction same-water-body constraint class.

Updated INDEX.md (Muscat → P1 Filled; added Salalah/Dhofar P2 Watch). Extended out-of-range-marquee-routes.md + grid-quality-by-market.md with Oman entries.
learnings:
  - methodology: **Sister-mesh wedge-ownership split convention** — when a city file mirrors 5+ cross-border routes with already-built sibling files, explicitly split wedge ownership by counterparty side (UAE files own UAE-counterparty wedges; Muscat file owns Omani-counterparty wedges). This avoids duplication while preserving completeness in each file. Generalizable to NEOM↔Aqaba (JO-side file when built), Singapore↔Batam (Indonesia-side when built), Miami↔Bahamas. Schema-level: recommend codifying as "**counterparty-side wedge ownership rule**" alongside the existing sister-pair routing table convention.
  - methodology: **"Single-point-of-leverage state developer" as a wedge archetype** — OMRAN (Oman state tourism developer) controls 5+ destinations (Al Mouj, Barr Al Jissah, Bandar Jissah, Jebel Sifah, Mina Sultan Qaboos, Yiti pipeline). One MOU = portfolio pilot ladder. Analogs: Aldar (AUH), Wynn-RAK (single property but design-in), PIF subsidiaries (NEOM/RSG/AMAALA but with separate conveners per giga-project), Khazanah/Themed Attractions Resorts in Malaysia. Worth a standing wedge-archetype prompt: "Is there a single state-developer counterparty controlling 3+ properties in the cluster?" — when yes, that's the highest-leverage pilot-ladder entry point.
  - methodology: **"Conservation-overlay class" as distinct from post-closure environmental overlay** — Daymaniyat is **born-protected** (UNESCO/MECA marine reserve), distinct from Boracay-style post-2018 IATF post-closure environmental overlay. Recommend schema enumerate three conservation/environmental overlay sub-classes: (a) born-protected (Daymaniyat, Ras Al Jinz Royal Decree, Komodo, Galápagos), (b) post-closure regulatory-corrective (Boracay), (c) greenfield environmental scrutiny (RSG / AMAALA / NEOM). Three-class taxonomy not yet in template.
  - methodology: **Quanta-LR quiet-mode + low-wake positioning lever** — Daymaniyat is the **sharpest single instance globally** of this positioning. Worth explicitly elevating in the schema's N30 implications prompt as a distinct lever (alongside greenfield-diesel-refuel and edge-of-Pioneer routine-timetable upgrade): "**Quanta-LR quiet-mode + low-wake at conservation-overlay endpoints** — sharpest at UNESCO/Royal-Decree marine reserves where conventional fast-ferry wake/noise is the conservation-permit gate."
  - methodology: **Seasonality-inversion as a future-cluster-split trigger** — Salalah/Dhofar Khareef Jun–Sep inverts Muscat winter-peak/summer-dead pattern entirely. Distance (600 nm) + seasonality inversion = two-cluster argument, not one. Generalizable: any sub-cluster >300 nm away from anchor AND with inverted demand season → standalone cluster file. Recommend schema enumerate this as a split-trigger heuristic.
  - source-discovered: **Oman = first non-GCC-supranational MENA jurisdiction in dataset** — cert-path geometry open question: does GCC mutual recognition (under Bahrain MTT first-of-class lead) cover Oman MoTCIT, or is Oman operationally separate? If separate, parallel sandbox-equivalent path exists. Worth resolving in research; either redundant (reciprocity) or a clean hedge.
  - source-discovered: **Three-tier intra-Oman cultural gradient** matches v2026-05-27 schema legal-zoned-three-tier refinement cleanly: permissive zoned (Al Mouj / Barr Al Jissah / Jumeirah / Sifah / Chedi / W licensed properties) → moderate (Corniche / Muttrah / public beaches) → conservative public default (inland / Sur public / Ras Al Hadd public). Friday observance stricter than UAE.
  - source-discovered: **Sultan Haitham post-2020 succession sensitivity** — adds Oman to the catalogue of "leadership-transition residual sensitivity" markets (NEOM CEO churn, Jakarta Prabowo settle-in, Bangkok Pheu Thai, Manila Marcos-Duterte split). ### 2026-05-27 — Bangkok + Phuket sister-pair retrofit (SEA, v2026-05-27 schema)
**Files retrofitted:** `world-map/regions/sea/bangkok-thailand.md`, `world-map/regions/sea/phuket-phang-nga-thailand.md`.

**Routes flipped Pioneer-out → Quanta-LR wedge (H2 2026+) — headline retrofit output:**
- **Bangkok ↔ Hua Hin** (~90 nm cross-Gulf) — Klai Kangwon royal-overlook quiet-mode positioning lever.
- **Bangkok ↔ Koh Samet (direct)** (~130 nm) — collapses Pattaya forced two-tier architecture.
- **Bangkok ↔ Koh Chang / Koh Kood (Soneva Kiri direct)** (~190 nm).
- **Phuket ↔ Surin** (~80 nm NW) — DNP seasonal closure + Moken community sensitivity.
- **Phuket ↔ Langkawi (MY)** (~110 nm S) — **cross-jurisdiction MY↔TH; flagged as CANDIDATE SEA-first Pioneer II routine cross-border #6 globally pending platform-spec evolution + MY/TH bilateral velocity. Currently Quanta-LR-only.**
- **Phuket ↔ Penang (MY)** (~180 nm) — cross-jurisdiction.
- **Phuket ↔ Koh Samui** (~190 nm round-peninsula) — Andaman↔Gulf-of-Thailand cross-coast connector (the only credible water-link between the two Thai resort universes).
- **Phuket ↔ Koh Phangan / Tao** (~210–225 nm).

**New cross-region constructs / patterns established:**
- **Anantara group chain wedge** — 5-country single-MOU portfolio spanning Bangkok Riverside + Hua Hin + Layan Phuket + Mai Khao Phuket + Si Kao Trang + Banana Island Doha + Sir Bani Yas AUH + future Kihavah Maldives. Cataloged as **highest-Series-B-narrative-leverage hospitality chain wedge documented to date.** Logged in `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md`.
- **Three-tier cultural gradient (Thailand)** — legal-zoned permissive + Buddhist holy-day F&B overlay + conservative-at-royal-adjacent-corridor. **Royal/lese-majeste overlay HIGH everywhere.** Cleanest SEA application of MENA's three-tier gradient framework so far.
- **Sister-pair routing table with NO water connector** — first sister-pair instance where the two files are linked only by national protocol envelope (Marine Department, BOI, TAT, royal protocol), not by water (Bangkok↔Phuket round-peninsula = OUT-OF-SCOPE air-only). Differs from Manama↔Dammam / NEOM↔Aqaba which had real water connectors.
- **Candela positioning rule applied** — per Jaideep, do NOT pursue head-to-head on Pioneer II short-route urban shuttle (Candela P-12 competitive). **Both files lead long-range / premium / Quanta-LR-anchored**. Bangkok: Quanta-LR cross-Gulf line-haul (Candela cannot reach). Phuket: Quanta-LR cross-border (Langkawi/Penang) + cross-coast (Samui) + hospitality density. This is the **canonical "incumbent-already-here, differentiate-on-platform-reach" positioning playbook** — applicable to any future market where Candela / Artemis / Sunreef already present.
- **Greenfield-microgrid Quanta-LR diesel-refuel positioning lever (Thailand instances):** Yao Noi (Six Senses), Surin (off-grid), Koh Kood (Soneva), Anantara Si Kao Trang. Phuket cluster now sits alongside Oman / RSG / AMAALA / NEOM / Maldives as a partial greenfield-microgrid market.
- **DNP (Department of National Parks)** is the consequential non-Marine-Department regulator on the Andaman — permit + zoned-anchorage + Similan/Surin May 15–Oct 15 hard seasonal closure. **First non-Marine-Department-primary-regulator catalog instance in SEA dataset.** Pairs with KSA RCRC (Red Sea Coast Guard) + Oman OMRAN destination authority.
- **Soneva Kiri (Koh Kood)** = highest-conviction Quanta-LR endpoint in Bangkok file (water-only-arrival ultra-luxe); coordinate w/ Dotan Maldives Soneva thread. **Six Senses Yao Noi** = highest-conviction Pioneer II hospitality endpoint in Phuket file.
- **Phuket ↔ Langkawi** flagged as **candidate SEA-first Pioneer II routine cross-border #6 globally** — extension of the MENA Manama↔Dammam / NEOM↔Aqaba / NEOM↔Sharm catalogue. **First SEA cross-border catalogue candidate.**
- **Greenfield charging-design-in windows narrower than MENA giga-projects** for Phuket — but Royal Phuket Marina expansion + Ao Po Grand Marina expansion are real open targets. Empirical pattern: brownfield-luxe-hospitality markets (Phuket, Hong Kong, Mumbai) have *narrower* greenfield windows than greenfield-giga-project markets (NEOM, RSG, AMAALA, World Islands Dubai). **Charging-architecture window-width correlates with greenfield-vs-brownfield-luxe market typology.**

task: Born-current Turkey single-national file (regions/europe/turkey.md) under v2026-05-28 schema. **First European market in dataset + first FACTORY-HOST market.** Four-node cluster (Istanbul/Bosphorus + Bodrum/Turquoise + Çeşme/Izmir + Antalya/Lycia). Densest TR/GR Aegean cross-border catalogue ever documented (4–5 candidate pairs in single bilateral). Updated INDEX.md (Turkey Queued→Filled) + appended out-of-range-marquee-routes.md + first-of-class-certification-regimes.md + grid-quality-by-market.md + historical-jetfoil-graveyards.md + in-archipelago-mro-clusters.md.
learnings:
  - methodology: **Factory-host market = new posture-class** — Turkey is the first market in dataset where Navier has manufacturing footprint. Reshapes pilot calculus: (a) local-production proximity collapses lead time + spares + warranty + local-content compliance overhead; (b) Türk Loydu sandbox-equivalent first-of-class cert posture given factory presence + Turkish-flag option ("European Bahrain"); (c) factory-specced variants (cabin/branding/regulator-mods) can be built-in not retrofitted = pilot-velocity advantage; (d) seismic-continuity strategic risk for facility-siting (distinct from operational constraint). Recommend schema add **"factory-host market"** as named posture-class with structured prompts (factory site + commercial lead identity + cert sandbox-equivalent posture + supply-chain radius + seismic/continuity risk).
  - methodology: **Federal vs. municipal political-counterparty split as structural sequencing channel** — Turkey has AKP federal (UDH, TURKLİM, KEGM, TL) vs. opposition-CHP municipal (İBB Istanbul/IDO, İzmir Metro/İzdeniz). Separate sequencing channels required; cross-leverage = perceived political alignment risk. Generalizable to any market with opposition-controlled major-city municipal PTA (likely applicable in PH Manila, parts of US, parts of India if/when scoped). Recommend schema add **"municipal-vs-federal political-channel split"** as standing Holds sub-prompt when present.
  - methodology: **High-friction-bilateral tag (NEW class distinct from flag-and-exclude)** — TR↔GR Aegean cross-border (4–5 candidate pairs) is politically sensitive but operationally feasible (NOT flag-and-exclude like KSA↔Eilat / TW↔China Kinmen-Matsu / KR↔NLL / Antalya↔RoC-Cyprus). Recommend schema vocabulary distinction: **flag-and-exclude (operationally excluded under flag-state policy)** vs. **high-friction-bilateral (operationally feasible but politically sensitive; sequence after counterparty cert + multi-jurisdiction-neutralizing counterparty)**. D-Marin both-sides marina ownership = canonical neutralizing-counterparty mechanism.
  - source-discovered: **D-Marin (Doğuş Group) = first non-hospitality-chain multi-region single-MOU chain-wedge candidate in dataset** — 12+ marinas across TR + GR + HR + MN + UAE (Dubai Harbour). Parallel to Anantara hospitality chain-wedge but in marina-chain class. Highest-leverage operationally-clarifying counterparty in Turkey. Recommend extending **chain-wedge prompt** in schema to explicitly include **marina-chain class** (D-Marin) alongside hospitality-chain (Anantara, Aman, Six Senses).
  - source-discovered: **TR/GR Aegean = densest short-hop cross-border mesh globally** — Kaş↔Kastellorizo 1 nm (shortest cross-border maritime distance documented globally) + Çeşme↔Khios 5 nm + Bodrum↔Kos 10 nm + Marmaris↔Symi 15 nm + Marmaris↔Rhodes 30 nm = 4–5 candidate Pioneer II routine cross-border pairs in single bilateral. **Denser than GCC Manama↔Dammam (#1) + Gulf of Aqaba 4-corner mesh.** Cataloged as #11/#12/#13 + bonus Symi + Kastellorizo in catalogue (all candidate, all high-friction-bilateral). First European cross-border catalogue extension.
  - source-discovered: **IDO Istanbul Boeing 929 jetfoil retired ~2005 = FIRST EUROPEAN JETFOIL GRAVEYARD catalogued**. Root cause: maintenance/spares cost on aging Boeing platform + unit-economics, NOT demand-failure (Austal catamarans took over directly). Quanta-LR/Pioneer II addresses root cause directly (modern composite + electric/hybrid = lower per-seat-mile maintenance). Resurrection lever: position as next-gen jetfoil refresh to İBB/IDO. Counterparty politically distinct from federal AKP (sequence carefully).
  - source-discovered: **Tuzla shipyard cluster = densest shipyard cluster in eastern Med** (~30 yards; Sedef, Sefine, Çeksan, Desan, Anadolu, Cemre, Selah, Gemak). If Navier factory is Tuzla-sited, factory IS the European + eastern-Med + MENA MRO anchor for Quanta-LR/Pioneer II. Cross-Mediterranean MRO coverage radius (Quanta-LR single-leg) covers: GR + CY + LB + IL + EG north + LY coast + south IT + Adriatic + Black Sea coasts. First European MRO anchor in dataset.
  - source-discovered: **Imperial-residence heritage-protocol overlay extends to Istanbul** (Dolmabahçe + Topkapı + Çırağan + Beylerbeyi + Vahdettin) — parallel to Tokyo Imperial Palace; "Imperial-residence-as-heritage" class is now a dataset-recurring sub-class of royal-overlook (Imperial-Tokyo + Ottoman-Istanbul; not active-Diwan-political). Worth schema-promotion as named royal-overlook sub-class.
  - source-discovered: **Turkey calendar-year FY Jan–Dec + national-day PR-blackouts (Republic Day Oct 29, Victory Aug 30, Sovereignty Apr 23, Youth & Sports May 19)** added. Ramadan procurement-bandwidth applies but consumer-activity not suppressed at tourism coast (Turkish secularism + tourism-economy). **Erdoğan/AKP single-party-stable post-2023 → 2028** = no rolling-coalition-volatility (unlike TH/PH/ID/KR-post-Yoon). Distinct from organizational-instability classes.
  - source-discovered: **Cyprus question = second instance of geography-fit-politically-gated within-single-file** (parallel to NEOM↔Eilat). Turkish-flag vessel can dock TRNC (Kyrenia/Famagusta); RoC ports (Larnaca/Limassol/Pafos) flag-and-excluded under TR-flag. Both within same Quanta-LR upper-cruise distance (~200 nm from Antalya). ### 2026-05-28 — Hospitality-archipelago retrofit batch (Maldives + Bali + Philippines)

**Files retrofitted (3):**
- `/agent/home/navier/world-map/regions/sea/male-maldives.md`
- `/agent/home/navier/world-map/regions/sea/bali-indonesia.md`
- `/agent/home/navier/world-map/regions/sea/manila-cebu-palawan-philippines.md`

**Routes flipped OUT-OF-SCOPE → Quanta-LR wedge (H2 2026+) — the headline output:**

*Maldives radial (single-gateway archipelago):*
- MLE ↔ Cheval Blanc Randheli (Noonu) ~81 nm (LVMH flagship)
- MLE ↔ Velaa / Joali / Kanuhura (Noonu/Raa/Lhaviyani) 54–81 nm
- MLE ↔ Six Senses Laamu ~135 nm (IHG chain anchor)
- MLE ↔ Park Hyatt Hadahaa / Ayada / Equator Village (Gaafu/Addu) 250–300 nm
- MLE ↔ Anantara Kihavah / Soneva Fushi / FS Landaa (Baa) 60–70 nm — Pioneer-edge → Quanta-LR comfort

*Bali / east-Indonesia arc:*
- Bali ↔ Lombok/Mandalika 55–70 nm (edge-to-Pioneer-out → in-range Quanta-LR)
- Bali ↔ Sumba (NIHI) ~210 nm (COMO chain anchor)
- Bali ↔ Komodo / Labuan Bajo ~250 nm (UNESCO Komodo NP + Aman/COMO/Ayana)
- Sumba ↔ Komodo intra-east mesh 100–150 nm (new east-Indonesia Quanta-LR mesh)

*Philippines (largest batch flip count):*
- Manila ↔ Boracay ~210 nm
- Manila ↔ Amanpulo ~195 nm (Aman flagship — flipped from "OUT OF SCOPE pitch trap")
- Manila ↔ Coron / El Nido ~270–290 nm
- Manila ↔ Cebu ~310 nm
- Manila ↔ Siargao ~480 nm (Quanta-LR upper-cruise — flag fuel/route headroom)
- Cebu ↔ Palawan ~250 nm (inter-cluster Quanta-LR mesh)
- Cebu/Bohol ↔ Siargao ~150 nm
- El Nido ↔ Coron ~70 nm (intra-Palawan loop opens Pioneer-edge → Quanta-LR routine)

**Competitor reframes (per v2026-05-28 rule 3a):**
- **Maldives — Candela 12 vessels in-market**: Reframed from "do not pursue head-to-head" → **head-to-head Pioneer II on short atoll-pass hops + lead Quanta-LR on >70 nm spokes Candela cannot reach**. Documented positioning gaps (range / hospitality-tier branding / Quanta-LR diesel-refuel charging-infra-independence). "Do not concede Maldives" principle written into file.
- **Bali — Candela in Thailand context-adjacent, no Indonesia deployment**: clean greenfield; urge urgency on hospitality HQ relationships. Positioning gap pre-documented for if/when Candela enters.
- **Philippines — no Candela deployment**: clean greenfield. **Austal-PH conflict reframed (rule 3a) from "market exclusion" → "shipbuilding-partnership evaluation, do not auto-disqualify"**: PH-local Austal facility evaluated per-engagement; FBMA + Tsuneishi + Austal-PH become parallel local-fab partner options.

**Other major changes applied across all three files:**
- Sub-cluster tables: added required columns (Role / Distance from anchor / Pioneer II bucket / Quanta-LR bucket / Counterparty jurisdiction / Charging-spec window / Pier-access archetype / What's distinct).
- Role taxonomy applied (anchor / hub / secondary hub / leisure spoke / industrial / MRO / HQ-only / OUT OF SCOPE pitch trap / FLAG-AND-EXCLUDE).
- Forced-two-tier architecture explicit (PH — no anchor-direct Pioneer II; Bali Padang Bai secondary hub for Gili Pioneer II).
- Single-gateway-archipelago radial convention applied (Maldives MLE; PH Manila).
- **Charging architecture split Pioneer II (pedestal-spec gate) vs. Quanta-LR (diesel refuel)** in every file — canonical greenfield-microgrid Quanta-LR diesel-refuel positioning lever called out as the single most important Series-B narrative lever for hospitality-archipelago files (sits alongside Maldives/RSG/AMAALA/NEOM/PH out-islands as cross-region pattern).
- Sea-state decomposed per sub-water-body (Maldives lagoon vs. kandu vs. open ocean; Bali Badung Strait vs. Lombok Strait ITF vs. Indian Ocean; PH Bacuit vs. Cebu/Bohol vs. Manila Bay vs. Tablas Strait).
- Atoll-pass / strait tidal-current scheduling constraint distinct from wave/swell (Maldives kandus, Lombok Strait ITF).
- Statutory blackout sub-classes covered: weather-trigger (PH NDRRMC Signal 3 full closure), cultural/religious provincial (Bali Nyepi 24 hr absolute; PH Holy Week), national-day PR-blackout (MV Jul 26 + Nov 3 + Nov 11; PH Jun 12 + Nov 30 + Dec 30; ID Aug 17), organizational-instability/rolling sovereign (MV Indo-Sino swing; ID Prabowo settle-in; PH Marcos-Duterte split + 2025 mid-terms + 2028 election), intra-coalition volatility (MV + PH explicit).
- Gateway-city reframe applied (MLE; Bali Sanur/Benoa for east-Indonesia arc; Manila for Quanta-LR line-haul + Cebu sub-gateway).
- Jetfoil graveyard checks: MV none (fresh); Bali gap (research item); PH explicit (Boeing/Kawasaki Jetfoil → Aboitiz SuperCat lineage; failure mode = unit economics + maintenance scale → Tsuneishi/FBMA localization is the resurrection wedge).
- Safety-as-wedge market explicitly tagged for PH (Dona Paz + Princess of the Stars) and Bali (Lembongan/Gili capsizings).
- BARMM Sulu / Tawi-Tawi documented as **FLAG-AND-EXCLUDE** (ASG security), not silent omission — aligns with NEOM↔Eilat / Salalah↔Yemen class.
- Wedge hypotheses now specify **platform required (Pioneer II / Quanta-LR / both)** and H2 2026+ availability for Quanta-LR wedges; Series-B narrative-value tag.
- Reference-file cross-links applied: `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md`, `sea-monsoon-and-blackout-calendar.md`, `sea-fiscal-calendar.md`, `in-archipelago-mro-clusters.md`, `first-of-class-certification-regimes.md`, MENA equivalent for legal-zoned-exemption parallel.
- Three-tier customary-custodian gate (LGU → resort → bangka/banjar) explicit at PH and Bali.
- IDR FX volatility USD/SGD denomination convention applied (Bali).
- Royal/state-overlook: MV not-applicable (republic); Bali Tirta Empul cultural-sensitivity + Jakarta Istana inland; PH Malacañang state-overlook on Pasig River = state-Diwan-equivalent operational protocol gate.

**Cross-cluster opportunities surfaced:**
- **Bali ↔ Philippines direct unlikely** (Sulu Sea security + Sangihe-Talaud BARMM proximity + ~700+ nm); even Quanta-LR pitch-trap. Treat as separate clusters.
- **Hospitality chain wedges cross all three** (Aman: Amankila/Amandari + Amanpulo + Amanwana; COMO: Uma Bali + NIHI Sumba + Maalifushi; Six Senses: Yao Noi + Laamu + Palawan-future + Kanuhura; Anantara/Minor International: Bali-adjacent + Kihavah + BKK/Phuket). **Hospitality-chain MOU strategy is the dominant cross-region pattern across SEA hospitality-archipelago files.**
- **Local fabrication triangulation**: Tsuneishi Cebu + FBMA Subic + Lundin Banyuwangi (Indonesia) + Sri Lanka MRO (Maldives) form a credible in-archipelago manufacturing+MRO mesh for SEA/Indian Ocean — cite `in-archipelago-mro-clusters.md` (likely needs back-fill).
- **Blackstone TMA Maldives template translates conceptually** to PH (SM/2GO consolidation play) and Indonesia (private-Phinisi/charter consolidation play) at much larger scale — asset-holder template is reusable narrative, not Maldives-specific.

**Reference-file gaps still unfilled (back-fill priorities):**
- `sea-monsoon-and-blackout-calendar.md` — MV section, PH section, ID section all need full back-fill (Thailand + Vietnam are done). MV: Iruvai/Hulhangu, Eid, Independence Day, Victory/Republic days, presidential cycle. PH: Signal 1–5 statutory cascade, Holy Week, Habagat/Amihan, Jun 12 Independence, Nov 30 Bonifacio, Dec 30 Rizal, May 2025/2028 election windows. ID: Nyepi (24 hr absolute Bali-only), Galungan/Kuningan, Lebaran/mudik, Aug 17, Prabowo settle-in.

- `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` — needs to absorb all 17 flipped routes from this batch with the Pioneer II distance / Quanta-LR status / counterparty owner / diesel-refuel-positioning hook.
- `in-archipelago-mro-clusters.md` — Tsuneishi Cebu / FBMA Subic / Austal-PH Balamban / Lundin Banyuwangi / North Sea Boats / Sri Lanka triangulation needs writeup.
- `first-of-class-certification-regimes.md` — MV (Transport Authority), ID (DGST/Kemenhub PT PMA + flag), PH (MARINA) Quanta-LR first-of-class timing.

**Schema sub-prompts that were unclear / could improve:**
- The schema's "OUT OF SCOPE — pitch trap" vs. "FLAG-AND-EXCLUDE" distinction needed an extra worked example for security-excluded geometries (BARMM); resolved here but worth promoting.
- The "within-Quanta-LR-spec but pitch-trap-geometry" class (e.g. Jakarta↔Bali, Manila↔Davao) is implicit in the template; worth making explicit as a distinct sub-row to avoid future retrofit ambiguity.

**Unpromoted-entry count: I added 1 entry to the unpromoted tail with this retrofit (Hospitality-archipelago batch). Recommend parent flag for promotion review after the next 1–2 hospitality-archipelago files (Seychelles? Tahiti? Croatia?) so the cross-region pattern (Quanta-LR diesel-refuel positioning lever + Candela coexistence framing + chain-wedge MOU strategy + three-tier customary-custodian gate + jetfoil-graveyard resurrection lever per-geography root-cause) gets baked into the template canonically.**

learnings:
  - methodology: **Sister-trio (not just sister-pair) routing-table pattern documented for first time.** Bangkok + Phuket + Samui share Thai-national protocol envelope (Marine Dept cert, BOI, TAT, Anantara chain, royal-protocol overlay) but operate as three independent water-clusters (Upper-Gulf / Andaman / Central-Gulf). Each file carries a sister-trio routing table with counterparty-side wedge ownership. Extends DXB+AUH sister-pair / NEOM+AMAALA+RSG sister-trio pattern to Thai context. **Worked cleanly; no duplication; cross-file references maintained.**
  - methodology: **Seasonality-inversion split-trigger applied a second time (after Salalah).** Samui's NE-Nov–Mar-rough / SW-May–Oct-calm pattern inverts both Phuket (Andaman SW-blackout) and global MENA pattern; partial inversion vs. demand peak (NE = high-season hospitality but engineering trough). Shoulder-season pilot-window framing (May/Jun/Sep/Oct) as the strongest combined utilization window. **Confirms Salalah-discovered heuristic as a cluster-split criterion** — when sea-state season + monsoon orientation differ materially from anchor cluster despite shared national protocol envelope, file standalone.
  - methodology: **No-royal-residence-at-sub-cluster verification as explicit confirmation step.** Samui/Phangan/Tao verified to have no royal residence (Klai Kangwon Hua Hin is mainland; Grand Palace + Wat Arun Bangkok). Royal-overlook constraint applies at national-protocol level only — staging-level lese-majeste constraint does NOT apply at Samui. Worth promoting as explicit verification step for any Thai sub-cluster file (and any monarchy market with discrete royal-residence geometry — UAE per-emirate, Brunei, MY per-Sultanate, Bhutan, Oman).
  - source-discovered: **Anantara group chain wedge expanded to 8+ Thailand-side properties** with Samui addition: Riverside Bangkok + Hua Hin + Layan Phuket + Mai Khao Phuket + Si Kao Trang + Lawana Samui + Bophut Samui + Rasananda Phangan, plus Banana Island Doha + Sir Bani Yas AUH + future Kihavah Maldives. **Single MOU = 5-country, 11-property+ chain.** Highest documented Series-B narrative leverage globally; promote to dedicated chain-wedge tracker reference file.
  - source-discovered: **Samui↔Phuket (~190 nm round-peninsula) = largest Thai cross-cluster Quanta-LR route; Samui↔Bangkok (~225 nm cross-Gulf) = second-largest.** Both flipped Pioneer-out → Quanta-LR wedge (H2 2026+). Adds Samui↔Hua Hin (~145 nm), Samui↔Koh Chang/Soneva Kiri (~280 nm upper-Quanta) to the Thai Quanta-LR catalogue. Update `out-of-range-marquee-routes.md`.
  - source-discovered: **Samui grid mainland-tether single-point-of-failure** (PEA undersea cable from Don Sak; 2020 multi-day outage). Pioneer II shore-charging carries cable-failure risk; Quanta-LR diesel-refuel eliminates dependency entirely. **Mainland-tethered-island grid risk** is a new sub-class of charging-architecture lever — applies to many island markets (Penang, Langkawi, Hainan, Jeju, Sicily, etc.). Worth promoting to `grid-quality-by-market.md` as a tagged sub-class.
  - source-discovered: **Tropical Storm Pabuk (Jan 2019)** = first Western Pacific tropical cyclone to make landfall on southern Gulf of Thailand coast since 1989. Just south of Samui (Pak Phanang/Nakhon Si Thammarat). Demonstrates real but rare cyclone exposure on central-Gulf coast — relevant to weather-margin framing for Samui cross-Gulf Quanta-LR routes.
  - source-discovered: **Phangan Full-Moon Party (Haad Rin) brand-misalignment overlay** — monthly cultural-event; Navier premium-tier framing must NOT visibly serve the Full-Moon Party crowd (brand-misalignment risk with Aman/Six Senses/FS/Anantara clientele). New class of "brand-misalignment cultural-event constraint" — distinct from demo dual-use windows (Songkran/Loy Krathong/F1/Singapore Maritime Week pattern). Worth noting as a schema sub-class.
  - source-discovered: **Belmond Napasai (LVMH) on Samui** opens LVMH brand-level cross-file coordination with Cheval Blanc Randheli Maldives + Aman portfolio — extensible to LVMH hospitality global cross-file at Sampriti / Vlad Doronin convener line.
  - source-discovered: **Surat Thani as mainland-gateway hub + Khao Sok NP adjacency** = Thai analog of Salalah-as-mainland-gateway-to-Empty-Quarter pattern. Premium ecotourism (rainforest + Cheow Lan Lake) extends Samui-cluster narrative into mainland adjacency without leaving the file's water-cluster.
  - schema-gap: **Sister-trio (3-file national-protocol-envelope-sharing) pattern** — schema currently documents sister-pair (DXB+AUH, Doha+Manama, Bangkok+Phuket) and sister-quad (UAE 5-emirate mesh). Thailand sister-trio (Bangkok + Phuket + Samui) sits between; **propose adding "sister-trio" sub-clause to sister-N-tuple section** with worked example (Thailand, NEOM+AMAALA+RSG already implicit).
  - schema-gap: **Brand-misalignment cultural-event constraint** (Phangan Full-Moon Party at Haad Rin) — schema currently treats marquee events as either pure blackouts (Ramadan, Songkran), demo dual-use opportunities (F1, boat shows, Maritime Week), or operational-hazard windows (Loy Krathong krathong flotilla, Ganesh idol immersion). **Propose new sub-class: brand-misalignment cultural-event** — operationally permissible but premium-brand positioning must visibly avoid. task: Langkawi-Malaysia standalone stub (promoted from malaysia.md sub-cluster — Wave D second instance after Lombok)
learnings:
  - methodology: **"Promoted-from-sub-cluster" sequence works cleanly when rich payload + parent file already documents the row.** Second consecutive Wave D instance (Lombok 2026-05-29, Langkawi 2026-05-29) confirms single-write authoring is reliable when (a) parent file's existing sub-cluster row enumerates anchors + (b) payload front-loads the new-counterparty stack (LADA + KIA + YTL + UNESCO Geopark) + (c) cross-border architecture stated explicitly. Apply to remaining Wave D candidates (Raja Ampat, Komodo/Flores, Banda/Maluku, Wakatobi, Riau-Batam-Bintan, Toba, Derawan, Anambas/Natuna, Karimunjawa).
  - source-discovered: **Payload-vs-canonical-fact discrepancy: Sultan-of-Kedah is NOT currently Agong.** Current Agong (XVII, 2024–29) = Sultan Ibrahim of Johor per `malaysia.md` (already promoted). Payload's claim "Sultan-of-Kedah concurrently Agong" is factually wrong. **Methodology lesson: when payload contradicts a previously-promoted reference fact, FAVOR THE REFERENCE FILE AND FLAG.** Resolved in Langkawi file by (i) sequencing pitch around Kedah-Sultan as STATE convener only (no Agong overlap currently), (ii) retaining the *pattern* "rotating-Agong leverage window" as schema-promotable independent of Langkawi-specific applicability.
  - source-discovered: **NEW pier-access archetype: "cross-border-immigration-gated pier-pair" (sub-50-nm micro-corridor)**. Canonical instance: Telaga Harbour (Langkawi MY-side ICQ) ↔ Bundhaya Pier (Koh Lipe TH-side ICQ), 12 nm — Pioneer II in-range cross-border with EXISTING fast-craft ICQ co-location regime that Pioneer II must slot into. Parallel instances pending: **Singapore↔Batam Nongsa Pura/Sekupang (~12 nm Pioneer II routine #11 candidate)**, **Singapore↔Bintan Bandar Bentan Telani (~30 nm)**, **Hong Kong↔Shenzhen Shekou (~15 nm; politically gated)**, **Miami↔Bimini (~50 nm)**, **NEOM↔Sharm/Hurghada via Tiran-area future** if/when bilateral, **Khasab↔RAK (already cross-border #4 routine — confirm ICQ archetype same class)**. **Schema-promotion candidate**: extend CITY_TEMPLATE pier-access archetype taxonomy with this sub-class, distinct from `slot-allocated public pier complex` (single-jurisdiction) and `per-pier concession matrix`. Convener stack: paired ICQ regulators (MY Royal Customs + TH Imm/Customs/Quarantine) co-located with paired pier-operators (Telaga commercial + Bundhaya commercial), gating Pioneer II routine timetabling on customs operating hours rather than range/sea-state alone.
  - source-discovered: **"Rotating-Agong leverage window" (NEW pattern, schema-promotion candidate).** In Malaysia's 9-state rotating-Agong system (5-year cycle), the state whose sultan is concurrently Agong carries elevated federal-royal-content rigor AND elevated state-developer/state-EDB convener-leverage. Worked instance: Pahang 2019–24 + Johor 2024–29 (Iskandar Puteri / Johor Bahru / Desaru sequencing rigor elevated 2024–29 per malaysia.md). Generalizable beyond MY: applies anywhere a federal/national monarchy rotates among sub-sovereigns — Brunei does not rotate (single-sultanate); UAE rotates Presidency among Rulers (Abu Dhabi Sheikh = President permanently since 1971 by convention — not the same pattern); Bhutan single-monarch; KSA single-monarch. **Currently uniquely a Malaysian schema element.** Recommend addendum row in `malaysia.md` standing rules + cross-link to per-state files for the active-rotation cycle.
  - source-discovered: **LADA (Langkawi Development Authority) = NEW Malaysian instance of `single state-developer portfolio` archetype** (v2026-05-28 section E). Joins canonical list (OMRAN Oman, Aldar UAE, Themed Attractions MY-Desaru, PIF per-giga-project, ITDC/InJourney Mandalika ID, Orascom El Gouna EG). LADA is a federal-state hybrid (analog to ITDC/InJourney rather than a pure state body); operates Langkawi Geopark Unit + tourism development + state-EDB hybrid. **Add to `reference/grid-quality-by-market.md` mainland-tethered-island catalogue + flag in time-bound greenfield charging-design-in window catalogue (Telaga Harbour + Royal Langkawi YC + Rebak Marina open windows).**
  - source-discovered: **YTL operates Datai + Andaman locally + Pangkor Laut + Tanjong Jara + Gaya Island Sabah + The Surin Phuket** = first-degree multi-property MY-side private convener with cross-file TH extension. **Add to `reference/hospitality-chain-wedge-tracker.md` as YTL row** (Malaysian-HQ chain, parallel to COMO SG-HQ / Banyan Tree SG-HQ / Anantara TH-HQ / Aman Sampriti-direct). YTL is structurally distinct from those tracker rows in that it's a luxury-hotel arm of a multi-sector conglomerate (utilities + property + hospitality), not a hospitality-pure chain — closer to LVMH-Belmond-Cheval Blanc pattern.
  - source-discovered: **"No-royal-residence-at-sub-cluster" verification (CITY_TEMPLATE section HH) confirmed CLEAR for Langkawi** — Sultan of Kedah's Istana Anak Bukit is inland (Alor Setar), no Langkawi-waterfront royal overlook. Parallel to Lombok's clear-HH-verification.
  - source-discovered: **Cross-border-immigration-gated micro-corridor cluster is OFTEN the lower-friction MY↔TH Pioneer II cross-recognition pilot venue than the headline 110-nm Phuket pair.** The 12–17 nm Koh Lipe / Tarutao / Adang micro-cluster has (i) existing fast-craft ICQ precedent, (ii) Pioneer II in-range with platform-spec headroom, (iii) lower bilateral-cert-velocity ask (familiar customs regime), (iv) born-protected eco-tourism narrative (Tarutao DNP). The 110-nm Phuket pair has higher narrative leverage but higher bilateral-cert friction. **Methodology lesson: when a market shows BOTH a headline cross-border distance (~110 nm Pioneer-out/candidate) AND a sub-50-nm immigration-gated micro-corridor, the micro-corridor is the cert-pilot venue; the headline route is the marketing wedge.** This pattern likely re-applies at SG↔Batam (12 nm pilot) → SG↔Bintan (30 nm) → SG↔KL/Penang (Quanta-LR marketing).
  - schema-gap: **No explicit pier-access archetype sub-class for "cross-border-immigration-gated pier-pair"** — had to introduce inline. Confirms section LL hub-radial archetype implies counterparty-side standalone files are MANDATORY, not optional — SG owns SG-side wedges, Riau owns ID-side wedges, no mirrored routing tables. **Generalizable pattern: every hub-radial declaration (LL) requires counterparty-side files for each counterparty market with ≥2 distinct sub-clusters reachable from hub.** For SG, that means Riau (done now), MY-peninsular (malaysia.md handles), TH (phuket.md handles). For future Dubai hub-radial declaration, counterparty files would be needed for Oman/Bahrain/Qatar/RAK/Fujairah sides (mostly already exist). For future Miami hub-radial, Bahamas/Cuba(deferred)/Cayman would be counterparty-side. **Schema-promotion candidate: explicit "counterparty-side companion file" requirement in section LL.**
  - source-discovered: **NEW canonical MM #4 sub-class: "foreign-state-developer-zoned"** — Sembcorp Development (SG state-owned Temasek subsidiary) operating Lagoi resort SEZ on Indonesian soil = first dataset instance of state-developer operating in foreign jurisdiction. Structurally distinct from same-jurisdiction state-developer (OMRAN Oman, Aldar UAE, ITDC Indonesia, LADA Malaysia, PIF KSA, Orascom Egypt — all single-jurisdiction). Implies a cross-jurisdiction sovereign-capital + foreign-regulator hybrid pathway. Likely parallels: Khazanah/Temasek/GIC cross-investments globally; future PIF cross-border resort SEZ; Mubadala in Mediterranean. **Promote as MM sub-class explicitly** + add to single-state-developer-portfolio archetype taxonomy.
  - source-discovered: **NEW politically-active-CAUTION sub-category — middle tier between routine and flag-and-exclude.** Natuna 9-dash-line PRC-friction zone is structurally distinct from the 5-instance flag-and-exclude catalogue (KR NLL + TW Kinmen-Matsu + PH BARMM + MY ESSCOM + ID Papua highlands) — routine commercial ops permitted, full Indonesian sovereignty asserted, but geopolitical-rigor elevated + TNI/Navy/KKP enforcement presence elevated + foreign-flag commercial operators face elevated scrutiny. Likely parallels worth checking: **Spratly-Paracel-adjacent Vietnamese coastal zones (Da Nang/Nha Trang ↔ Paracel sensitivity); Senkaku-adjacent Okinawa; Mediterranean Cyprus-EEZ-overlap zones; Crimea/Sevastopol; East-China-Sea-coast cities.** **Promotion candidate: extend `politically-active-flag-and-exclude-zones.md` reference file (when created per RA-learnings) to TWO sub-categories — flag-and-exclude (hard) and politically-active-CAUTION (elevated-rigor but routine-permitted).**

Promote to schema SS catalogue.
  - source-discovered: **`single-dominant-private-operator` near-monopoly counterparty class** — Wakatobi Dive Resort + Pelagian liveaboard (US-Swiss-owned since 1995, ~USD 400–800/night, private Maranggo airstrip with chartered Bali↔Maranggo air-link) = entire-archipelago marquee counterparty. Possible future-parallels: Six Senses Laamu Maldives (resort-island monopoly), Sonu Shivdasani at Soneva, Misool Resort RA (partial-parallel), Mnemba Island Tanzania &Beyond.

Statelessness-overlay diplomatic sensitivity + livelihood-transition pacing distinctive vs. Bali banjar / Maldives kandu / Hawaii hui / iTaukei. **Promote SEA-wide sea-nomad indigenous-protocol reference file from "pending pass #7 deferred" to "high-priority" — now appears with named-village specificity in 2 Wave D ID files (Wakatobi + Riau Orang Laut + Banda-Maluku Sama-Bajau adjacency)**.
  - source-discovered: **Pertamina MOR-VII Sulawesi-Maluku-Papua at Kendari + Makassar** = confirmed Quanta-LR refuel-mid-node candidates for SE Sulawesi fan + Wakatobi/Banda/Buton/Bali corridor (Wakatobi↔Bali 520 nm requires LBJ + Bima/Sumbawa or Lombok secondary mid-node per PP rule). Sister to MOR-VIII Wayame Ambon (Banda-Maluku-promoted) and MOR-VI/Pertamina Batam (SG-radial-promoted). **Add to `reference/quanta-lr-refuel-mid-nodes.md` candidate rows + flag Pertamina MOR-region-geography mapping convention** (MOR-VI Sumatra/SG-side, MOR-VII Sulawesi-Maluku-Papua-south, MOR-VIII Maluku-Papua-east).

Indonesian dormant-Sultanate inventory expanding — flag for `reference/indonesia-sultanate-convener-channel.md` (pending) to include dormant-roster alongside active-roster.

Extends VV catalogue: VV is now NOT solely Misool-canonical but a 2-instance class (Misool + Op-Wallacea Hoga).

Parallel candidate: Misool Sorong-charter, Soneva Maldives seaplane-tier, some Galápagos resort-charter. Sub-class candidate for hospitality counterparty taxonomy.
  - source-discovered: **MRO-deprivation moderate-tier** for Wakatobi at ~700 nm to Banyuwangi composite-MRO — better than Banda (1,000 nm) / RA (1,500 nm) / Ternate (1,500 nm); worse than Komodo (~450 nm); comparable to Lombok. **Three-tier inventory now visible**: severe-MRO-deprivation (>1,000 nm Banda/RA/N-Maluku) / moderate-MRO-deprivation (~500–700 nm Komodo/Lombok/Wakatobi) / low-MRO-deprivation (<500 nm Bali/Java-adjacent). learnings:
  - source-discovered: **IKN Nusantara new-capital-relocation in East Kalimantan is a NEW sovereign-anchor sub-class — `new-capital-relocation greenfield adjacency` — first instance globally in Navier dataset.** Otorita IKN = sovereign-developer with PRESIDENTIAL authority (Kepala Otorita reports directly to President), structurally distinct from ITDC/BPODT/Bappenas-line-ministry DPSP framework. IKN-Penajam↔Derawan ~340 nm Quanta-LR line-haul = compound government+tourism+sovereign-anchor marquee Series-B narrative ("from new capital to diving paradise in 4 hours"). **Promotion candidate to schema MM trigger #1 sub-class taxonomy + parallel reference file alongside `reference/indonesia-dpsp-framework.md`** (DPSP = Bappenas-line-ministry framework; IKN = presidential-authority direct — DIFFERENT sovereign architecture). Suggest: `reference/indonesia-ikn-nusantara-sovereign-anchor.md` companion file + future `ikn-nusantara-east-kalimantan-indonesia.md` HQ-only counterparty companion-file. Future-parallels: any future national capital relocation (Egypt New Administrative Capital, Myanmar Naypyidaw retrofit, Brazil Brasília already historic, Kazakhstan Astana already historic) — global watch class.
  - source-discovered: **ESSCOM eastern-Sabah politically-active flag-and-exclude HARD class canonically instantiated here** — Derawan↔Sandakan ~250 nm geographically Quanta-LR-fit but operationally excluded. **5th file in HARD catalogue (KR NLL + TW Kinmen-Matsu + PH BARMM + ID Papua highlands + MY ESSCOM eastern-Sabah)**; ESSCOM previously listed in schema WW (a) as catalogue-entry but now has canonical-instantiation file. Promote into `reference/politically-active-flag-and-exclude-zones.md` ESSCOM entry as canonical-fire-file (was previously schema-listed-without-canonical).
  - source-discovered: **Densest 4-Sultanate dormant-cluster in any Wave D ID file — Berau (extinct 1960) + Bulungan (extinct 1964) + Kutai Kartanegara (cultural-active Erau Festival) + Pasir** = parallel structure to N-Maluku active-4-Sultanate stack but in dormant register. **§CCC catalogue extension: East-Kalimantan dormant-4-Sultanate stack alongside Penyengat / Sisingamangaraja XII / Buton single-instance entries** — promotes CCC from 3-instance to 7-instance (4 single + 1 dense-cluster). **Kutai Kartanegara Erau-festival cultural-active register** is sister to Toba Sisingamangaraja XII active-cultural-but-not-administrative pattern — sub-class within CCC dormant. Pending `reference/indonesia-sultanate-convener-channel.md` dormant-roster anchor.
  - source-discovered: **Pertamina RU-V Balikpapan (Indonesia's 2nd-largest refinery) + MOR-VI Kalimantan-region = strongest Quanta-LR diesel-refuel supply-chain in Wave D ID dataset** (deeper than Pertamina Batam MOR-VI Sumatra / Pertamina Wayame Ambon MOR-VIII / Pertamina Kendari-Makassar MOR-VII). Confirmed-canonical refuel-mid-node for BPN/IKN/Samarinda/Tarakan↔Derawan 290–340 nm + onward Sulawesi/Maluku fan. **Promote `reference/quanta-lr-refuel-mid-nodes.md` Pertamina RU-V Balikpapan to CONFIRMED row** (sister to Penang + Pertamina Batam + Lombok). Also: **Pertamina MOR-VI = both Sumatra-side (Riau-promoted) AND Kalimantan-side (this file) per MOR-region-geography mapping convention** — need to clarify in reference file (likely two sub-regions within MOR-VI or naming discrepancy worth checking).
  - source-discovered: **§MM #3 cross-border-routing negative-fire = 3rd consecutive Wave D ID file** (Banda-Maluku #6 + Wakatobi #7 + Derawan #8). 3-of-8 Wave D ID files now non-cross-border on #3 — confirms EEE auditable-negative empirical pattern in Eastern-Indonesia + frontier-adjacent water-clusters. **Derawan adds the FRONTIER-ADJACENT-BUT-FLAG-EXCLUDED sub-pattern**: geographically Quanta-fit cross-border BUT operationally excluded by flag-and-exclude class — distinct from Eastern-Indonesia interior #3-N/A (Banda-Maluku, Wakatobi) which simply lack adjacent counterparty markets. **Two-sub-pattern within §MM #3 negative-fire**: (a) interior-no-adjacency (Banda-Maluku + Wakatobi); (b) frontier-adjacency-but-operationally-excluded (Derawan↔ESSCOM-Sabah). Worth EEE catalogue extension.
  - source-discovered: **Kakaban Lake stingless jellyfish = one of only 2 globally** (Palau Mlik being the other — Palau collapsed 2016, recovered). **Globally-rare conservation overlay sharper than typical KLHK NP single-overlay** but **NOT YET UNESCO-inscribed** — likely future UNESCO Geopark or Biosphere Reserve candidate. **§SS catalogue sub-class extension: `globally-rare-but-not-yet-UNESCO-inscribed conservation overlay`** — distinct from inscribed-WHS (Komodo) / inscribed-MAB (Wakatobi 2012) / tentative-WHS (Banda + Buton) / private-MPA (Misool RA). Sister candidates: Palau Mlik, future Aldabra-extensions, future Galápagos-extensions.
  - source-discovered: **Triple-environmental-ministry channel co-fire at SINGLE-CLUSTER scale** — KKP Berau Marine Park (2005) + KLHK Sangalaki Marine Tourism Park + BB-KSDA Kakaban Lake Marine Tourism Park = three simultaneous environmental tracks within Derawan cluster. **Sharper co-fire pattern than RA's KKP-RA + KLHK-Cenderawasih boundary-fire** (which is two-cluster boundary, not single-cluster co-fire). Promote `reference/first-of-class-certification-regimes.md` ID section: Derawan = ID's first single-cluster KKP-+-KLHK-+-BB-KSDA triple-co-fire instance. Sub-class extension within §UU multi-environmental-ministry first-of-class catalogue.
  - source-discovered: **Maratua Airport (MRT) ~2018+ short-strip resort-direct = resort-air-charter sub-class candidate** parallel to Wakatobi Maranggo (Tomia) precedent. **2-instance pattern now** within resort-air-charter sub-class (Wakatobi Maranggo + Maratua MRT). Differs from Wakatobi by being public-airport (not resort-owned) but functionally serves resort-direct demand.
  - source-discovered: **Ultra-greenfield ultra-luxury chain SLOT — Aman / Six Senses / Soneva / Banyan Tree / Belmond ALL absent from Derawan/Maratua/E-Kalimantan**. Distinct from Komodo/Lombok/Bali emerging-pipeline pattern AND from RA's Misool-independent-ultra-premium pattern. **Mid-tier-cluster (Nabucco USD150–400) + chain-absence = sharpest greenfield chain-MOU slot in Wave D ID dataset**. Sampriti-direct Aman-Indonesia mesh extension structurally fit (UNESCO/conservation overlay pattern matches Amanwana-Moyo precedent).
  - source-discovered: **Sangalaki Dive Lodge = single-property monopoly on Sangalaki turtle-nesting island** = single-dominant-private-operator sub-class candidate (Wakatobi-Resort precedent). 2nd-instance candidate within that class.
  - source-discovered: **Severe-MRO-deprivation tier (~1,250 nm Derawan↔Banyuwangi composite MRO)** — slots into Wakatobi-promoted 3-tier banding between Banda/RA/N-Maluku severe (>1,000 nm) and Komodo/Lombok/Wakatobi moderate (500–700 nm). **Confirms 3-tier banding is genuine** with Derawan = 5th severe-tier file. Balikpapan steel/aluminium commercial shipyards present (PT Patria Maritime, PT Tesco) but NOT composite-fast-craft capable — supplier-adjacency only, not MRO.
  - schema-gap: **CRITICAL NEW MM trigger #1 SUB-CLASS: `new-capital-relocation greenfield adjacency`** — first instance globally (IKN Nusantara). Recommend promote to v2026-05-30 addendum as explicit sub-class within counterparty-architecture distinction trigger AND as standalone sovereign-anchor archetype parallel to DPSP / single-state-developer-portfolio / cultural-sovereign-convener-stack / single-dominant-private-operator. Global future watch: Egypt New Administrative Capital (any analog), Myanmar Naypyidaw, Kazakhstan Astana (historic; precedent). **Strongly motivates: NEW reference file `reference/national-capital-relocation-sovereign-anchors.md` OR `reference/indonesia-ikn-nusantara-sovereign-anchor.md` as ID-specific instance**.
  - schema-gap: **EEE convention §MM #3 negative-fire sub-pattern extension**: (a) interior-no-adjacency vs. (b) frontier-adjacency-but-operationally-excluded (Derawan↔ESSCOM canonical). Promote to v2026-05-30 EEE clarification.
  - schema-gap: §SS catalogue sub-class extension: **`globally-rare-but-not-yet-UNESCO-inscribed conservation overlay`** (Kakaban canonical) distinct from inscribed-MAB / inscribed-WHS / tentative-WHS / private-MPA — 5th SS sub-class.
  - schema-gap: §UU multi-environmental-ministry first-of-class catalogue: **single-cluster KKP+KLHK+BB-KSDA triple-co-fire** (Derawan canonical) — distinct from RA's two-cluster boundary-fire pattern; sub-class clarification.
  - schema-gap: §CCC dormant-heritage-royal-overlook catalogue extension: **dense-dormant-Sultanate-cluster** (East-Kalimantan 4-Sultanate canonical) — sister to single-instance entries (Penyengat / Sisingamangaraja XII / Buton). Sub-class within CCC.

date: 2026-05-30
subagent: navier-city-dossier.md
task: Dubai city dossier — first dossier-class invocation (5 deliverables: dossier, planner-deck, platform-pitch (Careem), contact-targets, pilot-spec)
learnings:
  - methodology: First instance of the new dossier subagent. Single-city, 5-artifact format. Loaded canonical city file + platform-specs + shared principles only — total context cost ~60% of a stub run. Suggests dossier-subagent's read-list is well-scoped (no need to pre-load reference/*.md catalogue; city file already cross-references them).
  - methodology: For dossiers, the canonical city file at v2026-05-27 already contained ~90% of dossier dossier.md content as scaffolding (sub-clusters, players, wedge hypotheses, holds). Dossier dossier.md ends up being a re-styled re-section of city file + (a) hospitality top-10 ranking, (b) explicit risk register, (c) the bold-tagline wedge sentence, (d) sourced/needs-verification labeling discipline. The five-doc structure layered on top of a well-developed city file is genuinely high-leverage; for under-developed city files this subagent will fail or pad.
  - source-discovered: Specific Dubai pilot capex/opex bands worth re-using as MENA-baseline template (cabinet 80–120k, 50A overnight fallback ~70% route coverage, captain 7k/mo loaded, berth 2–4k/mo at Dubai Harbour Phase-1, P&I first-of-class 5–8k/mo). Different markets will deviate but Dubai is a usable centerpoint for any GCC dossier.
  - source-discovered: Career-stage observation — Pioneer II GMV math at 8 pax × 12 trips × 5 days × AED 500 → AED 240k/wk/boat is a defensible mid-case for premium-tier intra-cluster pilots (Dubai, Singapore SG-Sentosa, HK Victoria Harbour). Useful template for platform pitches across cities.
  - schema-gap: Dossier instructions did not specify a target word count for `planner-deck-outline.md` and `platform-pitch.md`. I treated them as ~10-slide outlines with 3–5 bullets + image + speaker note per slide (~1,200–1,500 words each). Subsequent dossiers should follow this density; add to instructions explicitly.
  - schema-gap: No template for the speaker-note style. I used 1-sentence directive ("anchor X early," "defuse Y," "lead with Z"). Worth canonicalizing in dossier instructions.
  - schema-gap: GMV math conventions — I labeled per-leg fare + utilization + ops-week count clearly as `[ASSUMPTION]` and built a sensitivity-table-implied range. Dossier instructions should formalize this (always 3-tier sensitivity: low/mid/high; always label assumptions inline).
  - schema-gap: Contact list `[gap]` names — instructions say honor blocked-item flags, but didn't say whether to expand `[gap]` placeholders or leave them. I kept `[gap]` (honesty principle from _SHARED_PRINCIPLES.md §4). Worth explicit dossier-instruction confirmation.
  - efficiency: Single-turn parallel writes for all 5 files worked cleanly (5 write_file calls in one turn). For future dossiers, batch all 5 writes after a single canonical-read turn — minimizes round-trips.
  - efficiency: Unpromoted-entry watermark below most recent PROMOTION NOTE (pass #7 + SEA/EA close-out 2026-05-29): Likupang + Brunei + this Dubai dossier = **3 entries — AT THRESHOLD.** Parent should consolidate (pass #8) before further dossier or city-stub fan-out.
suggested-update:
  target-file: /agent/subagents/navier-city-dossier.md
  change: |
    (1) Specify word/density targets for each artifact: dossier.md 1,500–2,500 (already stated); planner-deck-outline.md and platform-pitch.md ~1,200–1,500 words (10 slides × ~120–150 words each); contact-targets.md 15–25 rows in single ranked table; pilot-spec.md 800–1,200 words.
    (2) Speaker-note style: 1 sentence, directive ("anchor X," "defuse Y," "lead with Z"); never descriptive.
    (3) GMV math: always 3-tier sensitivity (low/mid/high); label inputs inline as [ASSUMPTION]; do NOT hardcode currency conversion without disclaimer.
    (4) Contact list `[gap]` policy: honor _SHARED_PRINCIPLES.md §4 — never fabricate names; `[gap]` with role + org is the correct treatment. Make this explicit in dossier instructions to remove ambiguity.
    (5) Explicit reminder that dossier subagent's READ scope is intentionally minimal (city file + platform specs + shared principles + learnings tail) — do NOT load `reference/*.md` catalogue except by deliberate need; city file cross-references them.

Suggest formalize: "rank by (Pioneer-II in-range coverage AND/OR Quanta-LR endpoint) × (named-decision-maker accessibility) × (signature-event proximity)."
