### 2026-05-28 — country-shell-split scout (japan/korea/taiwan/turkey/cambodia/malaysia/vietnam)

**Task:** Convert 7 country atlas shells into top-N coastal sub-city node proposals (planning pass only — no nodes.json mutation). Output: `/agent/home/navier/atlas-external/country-shell-split-plan.md`. **19 new nodes proposed** (Japan +4, Korea +3, Taiwan +2, Turkey +3, Cambodia +1, Malaysia +3, Vietnam +3).

**Learnings:**
- **Split-signal triangulation works:** sub-clusters that promote cleanly hit ≥2 of {single-developer-portfolio anchor (Sun/Themed-Attractions/Benesse/D-Marin/chaebol) + UNESCO/born-protected overlay + cross-border candidate}. Where all three co-locate (Ha Long, Setouchi, Penghu, Sabah, Bodrum, Phu Quoc) the split is *obvious*. Where only one signal is present (Phnom Penh urban-river, Yokohama intra-bay), keep folded into shell.
- **Cross-border candidates always promote.** Each candidate Pioneer II routine cross-border #6–#13 deserves its own node so the bilateral wedge has a permanent home (Bodrum/Çeşme/Antalya for TR-GR; Busan for KR-JP; Sabah for BN; Phu Quoc + Koh Rong sister-pair; Desaru for SG). The cross-border catalogue is now densest in Turkey (3–4 TR-GR pairs in one country) — first European cross-border-catalogue extension.
- **Country file's own wedge ranking is the cleanest split heuristic.** When a country file explicitly says "lead with X, defer Y, defer Z" (Cambodia: lead Koh Rong, defer Phnom Penh, defer Tonle Sap), promote only the lead. Resist the urge to split deferred sub-clusters just because intel exists.
- **Urban-river anchors stay as country shell** — HCMC, KL, Phnom Penh, Seoul, Taipei, Istanbul carry the regulator + ride-hail + chaebol/conglomerate convener relationships but the maritime product is usually thin (KL "DEPRIORITIZE" per file; Han River "structurally limited urban-mobility geometry"). Tokyo is the borderline case — kept as shell because the four out-Tokyo splits each anchor distinct wedge theses (Setouchi art-Triennale, Okinawa archipelago, Izu jetfoil-resurrection, Hokkaido winter-luxury).
- **Conservative posture countries split less.** Taiwan's cross-strait sensitivity → only 2 splits (Kaohsiung + Penghu), even though the file enumerates 5 sub-clusters. Better to under-promote in politically-fraught markets than to over-promote and have the planning ladder collapse on a flag-and-exclude row.
- **"Close call" splits worth defending in writing:** Hokkaido-Niseko (Niseko itself inland → promoted on Otaru/Iwanai coastal arrival-vector geometry); Izu-Shimoda (Tokai Kisen Boeing 929 graveyard-resurrection is canonical East-Asia jetfoil-graveyard root-cause case = own node for narrative ownership); Çeşme-Izmir vs. Bodrum (separable on shortest-cross-border-distance grounds = Khios 5 nm vs. Kos 10 nm); Antalya vs. Bodrum (Med-south Lycian + TAV gateway-city vs. Aegean charter capital — distinct wedge theses despite hospitality-cluster overlap).
- **Surprise: Turkey is unusually rich.** Single country generated +3 nodes (plus Bodrum locked) with 3–4 candidate cross-border pairs in one bilateral (TR-GR Aegean) — denser cross-border catalogue than any single SEA country. Factory-host market overlay further elevates split urgency (Tuzla shipyard cluster ≈ Phuket Boat Lagoon role for Med).
- **Surprise: Vietnam's Sun Group is the cleanest single-developer-portfolio archetype anchor in SEA** — controls 3+ properties in EACH of Phu Quoc + Da Nang/Lăng Cô + Ha Long Bay. First SEA analog to OMRAN Oman / Aldar UAE / Themed Attractions Malaysia. Each of the three Vietnam splits inherits Sun Group as the primary convener.
- **Flag-and-exclude rows never promote** — documented in deferred section for graceful-redirect framing but excluded from node count (Kinmen/Matsu PRC, NLL/DMZ, Côn Đảo Spratly, RoC Cyprus under TR-flag, Black Sea, eastern Sabah ESSCOM).
- **Anchor lat/lon best-guess flagging convention:** explicitly marked Koh Rong Samloem (10.71, 103.30) and Hokkaido Otaru (43.20, 140.99) as best-guess maritime anchors; OSM centroids used everywhere else. Parent should verify before committing to nodes.json.

## v8 atlas visual audit (15-city pass) — 2026-05-28

(a) **Bugs found across 15 cities**:
- **28 route stubs** across 9/15 cities (Muscat 6, Langkawi 5, Doha 4, Manila 4, Bali 3, RSG 3, Maldives 3, AUH 2, Bangkok 1). All same defect class: cross-cluster / cross-border destination coord resolves to anchor-jitter, line truncates within 1-2 nm of source anchor. v7 stub defect class **persists undiminished** in v8.
- **RSG anchor inland** at [36.85, 26], ~35 nm from coast — new instance of the v7 "Dubai-in-The-World" defect class; v7 fix was a Dubai one-off, didn't generalize.
- **HK = 0 routes** — entire HK route set missing from atlas.
- **POI `type` field universally collapsed to literal `"poi"`** — icon-type-collapse from v7 persists. Distinct `type` count = 1 for every city. Visual differentiation is colour-only (via `poi_class`), shape is always circle.
- **`anchor_node_id` + `parent_city_id` propagation broken** for the 15 boarding-point cities — 782/955 POIs (82%) have NULL anchor; coastal-POI v9 authoring (30-90+ POIs/city) DID land as geometry but did NOT populate the relational filter fields.
- **URL deep-link regression**: `?city=…` recognized but no camera flyTo on initial load (was working in v7); pill click still doesn't emit `?city=`.
- **9/15 target cities have no UI pill** (only Dubai, AUH, SG, Bali, RSG, Phuket pills exist).

(b) **Pattern across cities**: stub-count strongly correlates with **cross-cluster route ambition**. Cities whose route list is dominated by short intra-cluster radials (Phuket, Sharjah, Dubai) show 0-1 stubs; cities whose route list is dominated by cross-cluster / cross-border edges (Muscat→Khasab/Sur, Langkawi→KL/Sabang/Tarutao, Doha→Manama/AUH/Dubai/Dammam, Manila→Cebu/Palawan/Boracay, Maldives→Noonu/Gaafu) show stub-rates of 40-83%. Implies a **single root cause** (locale `parent_city_id` propagation broken → destination coord resolver fails → anchor-jitter fallback) that, when fixed, will collapse the entire stub defect class in one go.

(c) **Audit-protocol improvements I discovered**:
- **CRITICAL**: `?city={slug}` URL deep-link is currently broken (v8 regression). Subagent MUST use `map.jumpTo({center, zoom})` via `evaluate` instead of relying on URL navigation. Hard-coded fly-to coords needed per city. I'm appending the working set here:
  ```
  dubai [55.2708,25.2048] z=11   abu-dhabi [54.3773,24.4539] z=11
  singapore [103.8385,1.2906] z=11   bali [115.2167,-8.748] z=11
  phuket [98.338,7.9519] z=11   red-sea-global [36.85,26] z=9
  bangkok [100.5018,13.7563] z=11   hong-kong [114.1694,22.3193] z=11
  doha [51.531,25.2854] z=11   jakarta [106.8456,-6.2088] z=11
  langkawi [99.792,6.35] z=11   male-maldives [73.5093,4.1755] z=10
  manila-cebu-palawan [120.9842,14.5995] z=9
  muscat [58.538,23.6145] z=11   sharjah [55.406,25.3463] z=11
  ```
- **Atlas slug != audit-target slug**: source data uses verbose IDs (`dubai-uae`, `bali-indonesia`, `phuket-phang-nga-thailand`, `male-maldives`, `manila-cebu-palawan-philippines`). Audit MUST translate target slug → source slug for `anchor_node_id` / `from-to` filters or every count returns zero. Translation table is now in v8 reports — promote to v9 protocol.
- **POI `type` field is universally `"poi"`** — don't bother with `new Set(...features.map(f=>f.properties.type)).size === 1` check per city, it will ALWAYS be 1 with current build. Just assert at protocol start: "If global poi-type cardinality == 1, write a single global P1 line and stop checking per-city." Saves time.
- **Sub-cluster zoom pass (protocol §9) was unfeasible at 15-city scale** (15 cities × 3-5 sub-clusters × screenshot+evaluate = 60-75 round-trips, busting context). Recommend deferring sub-cluster pass to a SEPARATE subagent invocation dedicated to one or two cities at a time.
- **Screenshot-via-CDP requires `map.jumpTo` first, then `take_screenshot`** — relying on returned summary text alone misses visual defects (icon flatness, line-crossing-land, anchor-coord placement) that ONLY screenshot reveals. Don't skip screenshots even when source-data API gives counts.
- **The browser tool's `take_screenshot` writes attachment to `/agent/blocks/{blockId}/attachment_1.png`**, must be `cp`'d into the report folder — not a flag on screenshot itself.
- **Cluster badges (numbered circles) at z<11 obscure individual POI icons** — set z=11 for compact cities (Dubai, AUH, SG, Phuket, etc.), z=9-10 for atoll/archipelago cities (Maldives, Manila/Cebu/Palawan, RSG) accepting cluster occlusion in exchange for getting all sub-clusters in viewport.

learnings:
  - typology: **Hospitality-anchor + Yemen-arc flag-and-exclude overlay + intra-country Quanta-LR longitudinal arc** — 31 POIs (4 P0 / 6 P1 / 12 P2 / 9 hide). hide share 29% — second-highest in atlas after NEOM 27% (Salalah edges higher); reflects triple-overlay hide-load: (a) Yemen-arc flag-and-exclude-security TRIPLET (Sarfait western boundary region + Socotra route + Mukalla route — first 3-row Yemen cluster); (b) hide-protected dual (Al Baleed UNESCO + Khor Rori dual-overlay heritage+ecological); (c) Port of Salalah / Raysut / SFZ commercial-only TRIPLET (multi-counterparty government-port-complex authoring rule); (d) PACA cyclone+Khareef operational-overlay hide (analog to NEOM Northern Aqaba EEZ region-overlay). P0 share 13% — below hospitality-anchor 15-25% benchmark; correct given the closed-greenfield-leaning Hallaniyat pipeline is P2-status:planned and Yemen-arc consumes hide rows; "open today" filter does P0 lift via Hawana + Anantara Al Baleed + Alila Hinu + Hallaniyat-anchorage-village.
  - **Hawana Salalah Marina = 8th GCC class-1 greenfield charging-design-in window** confirmed alongside Sindalah / Qetaifan Lusail / BMDC Manama / Lusail Marina / Wynn Al Marjan / AMAALA YC / Jeddah Central / Hudayriyat. Distinguished by Orascom (Sawiris) + OMRAN dual-sovereign overlay — single MOU pulls Egyptian El Gouna/Taba Heights + Omani OMRAN-portfolio simultaneously. **First atlas instance of cross-country Orascom-Sawiris counterparty leverage** (Sawiris counterparty spans EG Red Sea + OM Dhofar — Pan-Arab Sawiris MOU surface).
  - **Yemen-arc flag-and-exclude TRIPLET** (Sarfait western boundary region + Socotra route + Mukalla route) — **first 3-row hide-flag-and-exclude-security cluster in single file**. Pairs with NEOM dual (Eilat route + Northern Aqaba region), Jeddah single (Port Sudan), Manila Sulu/BARMM single — Salalah is densest. Confirms pass-#9 universal extension + extends RAK Tunbs DUAL geographic-region pair sub-pattern to ROUTE+REGION+ROUTE triplet (Sarfait region + 2 destination routes). Pattern: when a city sits on a security-overlay-frontier with multiple destinations gated by the SAME conflict overlay (Yemen civil war + Houthi), enumerate ROUTE-PER-DESTINATION + REGION-OVERLAY together. **NEVER weaponize in writing** notes-discipline (per Sharm safety-as-wedge inversion convention) embedded inline at each Yemen entry.
  - **Khor Rori DUAL-OVERLAY hide-protected** (heritage UNESCO + ecological IBA) — first atlas instance where SAME POI carries BOTH hide-protected-heritage AND hide-protected-ecological. Confirms hide-protected-ecological/heritage split (pass-#9 promoted) can be authored as composite when site geometry hosts both. Propagation candidates: Tubbataha (cultural+ecological — heritage thinner), Komodo NP (UNESCO + ecological — already heritage-leaning), Galapagos.
  - **PACA Arabian Sea cyclone + Khareef monsoon DUAL operational-overlay hide** authored as single structural entry (analog to NEOM Northern Aqaba EEZ region-overlay + Sharm Sinai-interior envelope-clarification). Khareef is UNIQUE-IN-ARABIA seasonality-inversion (SEA-monsoon analog applied to MENA) — first MENA file with explicit monsoon-blackout flag (Jun-Sep). Cyclone corridor adds pre/post-Khareef secondary blackouts (Mekunu/Luban/Shaheen tracked). **Append to mena-blackout-calendar.md statutory weather-trigger section** per city file flag.
  - **Intra-country Quanta-LR longitudinal arc surfaced via Duqm pointer** — Salalah ~300nm Duqm ~300nm Muscat = TWO Quanta-LR upper-cruise hops to traverse Oman's coast. Duqm SEZ entry (SEZAD/OPAZ + ASYAD + ODC) adds a THIRD sovereign counterparty channel to Oman alongside MoTCIT regulator + OMRAN sub-sovereign — non-OMRAN sovereign-developer lever for intra-Oman Quanta-LR demos. First atlas instance of intra-country mid-arc staging-point as cross-cluster pointer (vs. cross-border or cross-cluster-archipelago).
  - **Mumbai Quanta-LR aspirational pointer** (~960nm) = first atlas POI explicitly authored as BEYOND-current-envelope NARRATIVE arc (Arabian Sea longitudinal Salalah↔Hallaniyat↔Mumbai↔Goa↔Maldives). Pattern: pointer-only narrative arcs can be authored at P2 with explicit "NOT FOR ROUTINE" notes to preserve atlas-narrative completeness for marquee positioning conversations without operational commitment.
  - **gateway-concentration POI ABSENCE wedge — 13th universal confirmation** (SLL Salalah Airport ↔ Hawana ~12km road, no marine handoff). Lighter magnitude than KAIA Hajj / HIA — closer to BIA Manama scale — but Khareef-season GCC summer-escape pax surge creates the real demand. OAMC + Muriya concession framing.
  - **public-water-mobility ABSENCE wedge — 7th universal confirmation** (Salalah Corniche / Al Haffa). Dhofar Municipality + MoTCIT RFP open. MENA-universal-except-Dubai pattern reaffirmed.
  - **Anantara MENA chain-wedge confirmed 7th property** (Doha Banana + Sir Bani Yas AUH + Maldives Kihavah + Hua Hin + Bangkok Riverside + Mai Khao/Layan Phuket + Al Baleed Salalah). Single Minor International MOU pulls 7 MENA+SEA properties.
  - **Multi-counterparty government-port-complex TRIPLE-SPLIT** applied to Port of Salalah cluster (APM container hide + Raysut industrial hide + SFZ marine interface hide) — confirms 3-way split per pass-#9 rule (Tanjung Priok canonical 3-way; Salalah 4th 3-way after Old Doha Port + Jeddah Islamic Port).

date: 2026-05-28
subagent: navier-coastal-poi.md
task: coastal-poi koh-samui-thailand (Samui + Phangan + Tao + Ang Thong NMP + Surat Thani gateway + 5 cross-cluster pointers Bangkok/Phuket/HuaHin/SonevaKiri/SG)
learnings:
  - typology: **Hospitality-anchor + Multi-cluster archipelago hybrid (Gulf-side counterpart to Phuket)** — 43 POIs (10 P0 / 15 P1 / 13 P2 / 5 hide). hotel_jetty share 47% — above 40% hospitality-density flag, confirming hotel-group-MOU-led counterparty stack (Anantara/Minor 3-property Samui-cluster wedge Lawana+Bophut+Rasananda; Marriott 4-property W+Vana Belle+Le Méridien+Centara-adjacent; Hilton-Conrad single flagship; IHG Six Senses+InterContinental Taling Ngam; LVMH Belmond Napasai; Banyan Tree Singapore-HQ). Sits at intersection of AUH 43-POI hospitality-anchor benchmark and Bali/Phuket multi-cluster-arc 50–70 benchmark; Samui's smaller absolute scale (3 islands + NMP) puts it cleanly at 43.
  - **MONSOON-COMPLEMENTARITY wedge structurally authored** as a dedicated POI entry (Samui NE-monsoon Nov–Mar blackout INVERTED from Phuket SW-monsoon May–Oct blackout). First atlas instance where the **complementarity** itself is a POI — Phuket+Samui seasonal-pair gives Thailand YEAR-ROUND Navier ops (national sister-trio complementarity wedge). Suggests promotion of "**seasonal-complementarity-pair POI**" as new authoring discipline alongside operational-overlay hide. Propagation candidates: future Goa SW-monsoon vs. Bay-of-Bengal NE-monsoon pair, Vietnam coast (Da Nang NE vs. Phu Quoc SW), Mediterranean E-W storm pairs.
  - **hide-water-body NEGATIVE-CONFIRMATION with seasonal+fishing-fleet hybrid caveats** — Gulf of Thailand authored clean re. river-outflow / hyacinth / perennial debris (distinct from Jakarta/Manila/Mumbai positive corridors) BUT with 4 documented caveats: (a) fishing-net flotsam baseline (Surat Thani fleet density); (b) monsoon-flushed terrestrial debris post-Oct; (c) Full-Moon-Party-night localised debris at Haad Rin; (d) Pabuk-2019 typhoon institutional memory. **Adds 6th sub-variant to hide-water-body class set**: arid-coast-clean (Doha/Bahrain/NEOM) / hybrid-seasonal (Jeddah Hajj-flotsam) / industrial-localised (Khor Khwair RAK) / operational-strait-gate (Tiran) / positive-perennial (Jakarta/Manila) / **+ fishing-fleet-baseline-with-monsoon-flush (Samui)**. Distinct from Jeddah hybrid in that the seasonal flush is monsoon-driven rather than pilgrimage-driven; distinct from positive-perennial in that baseline is clean enough to not gate routine ops.
  - **SOFT-LANDING-ONLY beach POIs as a new variant** — Chaweng main, Lamai main, Maenam authored as beach_club_jetty (no formal pier) with explicit "soft-landing-only operational constraint." First atlas instance where the densest tourism strip has NO formal pier (regulatory + beach-erosion gate). Suggests sub-class of beach_club_jetty: **mass-tourism-soft-landing** distinct from luxury-beach-club-private-jetty (Phuket east-coast clubs). Propagation: Pattaya beachfront, Goa beaches, Costa del Sol, future Boracay White Beach (already partially authored).
  - **Cape Fahn dual-islet hotel_jetty** = first atlas instance where the HOTEL IS the ISLAND (Madsum + Tan privately-leased islets accessed only by tender from Choeng Mon). Canonical Pioneer II Navier-Experience showcase asset where boat-only access IS the product. Pattern: distinct from standard hotel_jetty (resort-with-jetty) — this is **island-as-property** sub-pattern. Propagation candidates: Soneva Fushi (Maldives, single island), Cempedak/Nikoi Bintan, Banwa Pawikan PH, Calala Cay Nicaragua, future Seychelles private islands.
  - **Full-Moon-Party brand-misalignment SOP** embedded directly in Phangan Haad Rin pier + Big Buddha pier notes (premium-brand-PR overlay; Navier framing NEVER visibly serves FMP crowd; demo cadence avoids FMP nights). First atlas instance of monthly cultural-overlay PR-SOP embedded in POI notes (distinct from Sharm safety-as-wedge "NEVER weaponize" inversion — this is brand-positioning rather than scar-tissue). Propagation: future Ibiza superclub overlay, Mykonos, Tulum Halloween/NYE.
  - **gateway-concentration POI ABSENCE wedge — LIGHTER VARIANT** (USM Samui Airport seaplane_base planned). Per payload guidance: "tighter than Phuket — flag the lighter absence." Distinct from KAIA-Hajj / HIA / BIA / LGK / CGK heavyweight-absence instances in that USM already has marine-link hotels adjacent at Bang Rak (~3 km road) — absence is OPERATIONAL not infrastructural. Bangkok Airways is the airport private operator (not a government agency) — concession framing is private-counterparty-friendly. **14th universal confirmation of gateway-absence pattern, first LIGHT-magnitude variant**. Suggests sub-class refinement: heavyweight-absence (KAIA/HIA scale) vs. light-absence (USM scale) — the wedge still authorable but unit-economics calibration different.
  - **Soneva Kiri ~280 nm Quanta-LR upper-cruise pointer** authored as cross-cluster pointer to Koh Kood NE Gulf (counterparty endpoint in bangkok-thailand.md). Pairs structurally with Soneva Fushi/Jani Maldives (resort-microgrid solar-flagship class 7 charging). Soneva chain wedge spans Maldives + Thailand NE Gulf; Dotan cross-coordination thread. **Largest single-chain cross-cluster wedge in Thai-Gulf atlas** — single Soneva MOU pulls 2 cross-cluster Thai-side Quanta-LR endpoints plus 4 Maldives resorts.
  - cross-cluster pointer tiering: all 4 Thai cross-cluster pointers (Bangkok / Phuket / Hua Hin / Soneva Kiri) authored P2 per wedge-thesis-not-distance rule (all H2 2026+ Quanta-LR dependency; counterparty-side ownership in sister files). Singapore authored as hide (pitch-trap reference, P3/watch per payload). Distinct from Sharm's 4-pointer fan-out where NEOM-Sindalah retained P0 due to counterparty-side ownership rule with Pioneer II routine cross-border. Samui has NO Pioneer II cross-cluster route (all 4 are Quanta-LR-only) — relevance ceiling is P2 by structural definition.
  - **Multi-counterparty per-pier per-operator scheduled-network** (Lomprayah/Seatran/Raja/Songserm 4-operator × ~8-pier mesh) authored as SUMMARY ROW at Bang Rak representative coord per pass-#9 (N stops + multi-operator BUT >30 stops → summary; here 4-operator × 8-pier = 32 op-pier-pairs but pier endpoints already separately enumerated as Bang Rak/Maenam/Nathon/Lipa Noi/Thong Sala/Haad Rin/Mae Haad/Don Sak). **Hybrid pattern**: per-pier endpoints fully enumerated as primary POIs + single network-summary row layered on top — first atlas instance of hybrid network authoring. Distinct from HK Central Piers (per-pier-per-concession 8-row) and Dubai RTA (single-summary-row). Useful for ferry-incumbent-upgrade narrative.
  - **MRO pointer to Phuket Boat Lagoon (~190 nm Quanta-LR)** authored as out-of-anchor-radius shipyard_partner per pass-#9 extension rule — Samui has NO local large-MRO; nearest = Phuket Boat Lagoon; fallback = Bangkok Marsun/Italthai ~225 nm. **8th shipyard-partner-pointer instance** (extends Indonesia/Qatar/UAE-Sharjah/PH/Bahrain/NEOM-Oxagon/RAK-UAQ to Phuket-from-Samui-side). Confirms in-archipelago-MRO-clusters.md cross-reference works structurally as POI surface.

  - **Misool Resort + Misool Foundation DOUBLE-OVERLAY (UNESCO MAB Biosphere + privately-initiated MPA no-take-zone since 2007)** authored as TOP single-property wedge — **likely SHARPEST UNESCO-biosphere + Quanta-LR quiet-mode positioning instance GLOBALLY** (vs. Komodo single-overlay, Daymaniyat/Hallaniyat single-overlay). ### 2026-05-28 — atlas build.py v9 POI glyph rendering
domain: build-pipeline / visual-encoding
category: schema-gap + source-discovered
context:
  Jaideep flagged that POI pins were visually undifferentiated — v7/v8 used `circle-radius` + `circle-color` keyed on `bp_type` only. Two structural issues found and fixed:

  1. **Dual-source key-space mismatch.** Curated boarding-points/*.json POIs carry `bp_type` (underscores: `hotel_jetty`). Upstream nodes.json POIs (427 of them) carry `poi_class` (hyphens: `hospitality-hub`, `anchor-marina`, `out-of-range-marquee`, …). The v8 BP_COLOR expression only matched `bp_type`, so every nodes.json POI rendered as the generic blue default — exactly the "bp_type flattened to generic poi" symptom the v7 audit flagged, but the root cause was *not* a property-drop in the GeoJSON builder (props do flow through correctly at build.py:74 and :172); it was the *style expression* ignoring the parallel key. Fix: `BP_KEY = coalesce(bp_type, poi_class, 'default')` shared by BP_COLOR, BP_GLYPH, BP_SIZE; underscore and hyphen variants both listed in each match.

  2. **Glyph-server emoji coverage.** The MapLibre style points at `demotiles.maplibre.org/font/...` which ships Noto Sans Regular/Bold only — emoji codepoints (🚢 🏨 🛥 🛬 🚤 from the original spec) would render as tofu. Substituted Noto-Sans-safe Unicode (Geometric Shapes + Misc Symbols block): ⚓ ▲ ★ ■ ✦ ◆ ⬢ ⬟ ●. Verified within Noto Sans Bold's PBF range at the demo server.

methodology:
  - Always grep upstream node schema before writing style expressions — both `bp_type` (curated) and `poi_class` (nodes.json) key spaces exist and are not synonyms.
  - Before specifying emoji glyphs in MapLibre styles, check the glyph server's font coverage — demo/CDN stacks rarely include emoji. Default to Geometric Shapes / Misc Symbols for portability.
  - "v7 audit said X was dropped" deserves a trace, not a blind re-plumb — the propagation was fine; the consumer was the bug.

### 2026-05-28 — dossier→atlas enrichment pipeline (batch-3 retrofit)
domain: build-pipeline / data-spine
category: schema-gap + source-discovered
context:
  ~580KB of batch-3 dossier prose (city-dossiers/{6 cities}/*.md, 5 deliverable
  types each) was being consumed only by the Dossier Instant App; none of its
  named humans, RFP/MOU language, intro-path recommendations, or posture flags
  were reaching the atlas data spine (humans.json / orgs.json / side panels).
  Parent flagged the gap and asked for a pipeline step. Built
  `extract_dossier_intel.py` (5/6) + `merge_dossier_into_atlas.py` (6/6) wired
  into build.sh after canonicalize_orgs.py. Read-only on city-dossiers/.

  Initial run: 153 humans + 142 orgs extracted → 34 humans enriched + 119
  added; 72 orgs enriched + 34 added. Net atlas humans went from 510 → 629
  (+23%); canonical orgs gained dossier_notes on 72 entries.

methodology:
  - **Dossier markdown structure is highly regular** — every city
    contact-targets.md uses an 8-column pipe table (`#|Name|Role|Org|Why|Best
    path|W-C|Flag`), with header variants only in column 1 (#/Rank), column 6
    (Best path / Best path to intro), and column 7 (Warm/Cold vs W/C). A
    column-name detector keyed on substrings + a `most-recent-header-before-
    separator-row` parser handled all 6 cities + Phuket's split Tier-1/Tier-2
    sub-tables without per-city special casing.
  - **`[gap]` rows are not throwaway** — the dossier convention frequently
    packs role+org hints inside the brackets (`[gap — Careem exec]`,
    `[gap — Dubai Harbour leasing/berth director]`). A regex pulling the
    post-em-dash hint and folding it into the role string preserves
    actionability without inventing names. Maintains shared-principles rule #4
    (never fabricate).
  - **Two-pass matching ladder for humans** (confirmed name → city+name; gap →
    city+org slot with `[gap]` target; otherwise append) cleanly handles the
    mixed-status corpus. Critical detail: an in-process `_dossier_used` flag
    on each target prevents two dossier rows from claiming the same existing
    gap slot when a dossier has multiple sub-roles at one org.
  - **Per-city scoping for org enrichment** — appended `dossier_notes[]` as a
    list of `{city, notes, source_paths}` blocks rather than flat strings, so
    one canonical org (Uber, Grab, Aman, COMO, IHG) accumulates a per-city
    ledger as more cities land. Mirrors the existing `city_presence` shape.
  - **Substring org fallback (≥4 chars, both directions) was necessary** but
    is still too loose-or-tight in mixed cases ("Marriott Luxury Group" vs
    canonical "Marriott International" does NOT match; "Six Senses" vs
    canonical "IHG (with Six Senses, Regent)" DOES via parenthetical raw_name).
    Suggests a longer-term canonicalize_orgs.py alias-table expansion.

source-discovered:
  - City-slug skew between dossier folder names and atlas city slugs requires
    an explicit map (dubai → dubai-uae; phuket → phuket-phang-nga-thailand;
    red-sea-global → red-sea-global-ksa). This map should live in ONE place;
    today it's duplicated in extract_dossier_intel.py CITY_SLUG_MAP and is
    implicit in atlas-side parse_city_files.py via filename → slug. Promote to
    a shared `/agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/CITY-SLUG-MAP.json`.
  - The atlas humans.json uses `name_status` ∈ {confirmed, TBC, gap} but the
    dossier corpus has a 4th state: "confirmed but bracketed-uncertainty"
    (`Sheikha [Maktoum branch — full name TBC]`, `John Pagano` with parenthetical
    confidence). Today these get bucketed as `confirmed` by GAP_RE; should be
    a distinct `partial` status when the name contains a `TBC` token.

efficiency:
  - Sentence-grained dossier-prose scan for org context is cheap (~150 ms per
    city) but produces 2–3-sentence snippets when sentence-split fails on
    bullet lists. Paragraph-grained scan would be cleaner; defer until merge
    output is consumed by the UI and we know what density it tolerates.
  - Long auto-generated IDs (`dossier__dubai-uae__atlantis-dubai-kerzner-
    general-manager-atlantis-the-royal-palm-gm`) are functional but ugly.
    Truncating to slug(name|org)[:50] would keep collision risk low and
    deep-link URLs readable.

suggested-update:
  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/SCHEMA.md  (or new section)
  change: |
    Document the dossier_* field family added by stage 6/6:
      humans: dossier_context, intro_path, dossier_posture, dossier_flag,
              dossier_source, dossier_source_line
              source ∈ {existing, "dossier"} (new value)
      orgs:   dossier_notes[] = [{city, notes[], source_paths[]}],
              category ∈ {existing, "dossier-mentioned"} (new value),
              source = "dossier" (new value)
    Note the canonical city-slug map (dossier folder ↔ atlas slug) and pin
    it as the contract — any new dossier city must add a CITY_SLUG_MAP entry.

  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/canonicalize_orgs.py
  change: |
    Add 3 alias clusters that the dossier merge surfaced as gaps:
      - Marriott Luxury Group / Marriott Lux Collection / Ritz-Carlton Reserve
        → Marriott International
      - Yango → Yandex (already partial; add explicit raw_name aliases)
      - Bulgari Resort / Bulgari Hotels & Resorts → LVMH Hotels
    Each currently lands as a NEW org via the dossier merge instead of
    enriching the canonical entry.

  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/parse_city_files.py
  change: |
    Expand `name_status` vocabulary to {confirmed, TBC, partial, gap} where
    `partial` = name contains a "TBC" marker or bracketed-uncertainty (e.g.
    "Sheikha [Maktoum branch — full name TBC]"). Today these mis-classify as
    confirmed. The dossier extractor should mirror this convention.

### 2026-05-28 — data-spine v9 pre-build fix (parent_city_id audit follow-up)
domain: build-pipeline / data-spine
category: source-discovered + bug-fix

context:
  v8 atlas visual audit (15-city) named `parent_city_id` propagation as the
  single root cause behind 28 route stubs + RSG-inland + HK-0-outbound +
  82% NULL `anchor_node_id` + URL deep-link regression. Parent dispatched a
  surgical-fix subagent.

methodology:
  - **The "82% NULL anchor_node_id" framing was a measurement artifact.**
    Current build shows 0/427 NULL — parse_city_files.py line 284 sets
    `anchor_node_id = file_slug` for every synthetic POI. The audit was
    against a stale build OR a different node subset (probably locales-
    qua-POIs which DO have parent_city_id from extract_locales.py, also
    set correctly). Verify current-state metrics before believing a per-
    centage in a prior audit report — the *defect description* may still
    point at the real bug while the *symptom metric* is stale.
  - **Real defect is destination-coord propagation, not source.** The
    `parent_city_id` source-side propagation works. But parse_city_files.py
    treats EVERY sub-cluster row — including rows that describe external
    destinations ("Dubai (UAE — cross-border)", "Salalah / Dhofar",
    "Doha ↔ Manama (Bahrain)") — as a synthetic POI child of the source
    city. With no OSM hit, those POIs fallback-jitter around the source
    anchor → edge derived from `distance_nm_from_anchor` renders as a
    stub. The fix is a destination-resolver second pass, not a source-
    propagation patch.
  - **Cleanest fix location: resolve_cross_file_edges.py (downstream of
    parse).** It already builds a city-alias + POI name index for the
    prose-↔ pass; reusing that index for synthetic-POI re-resolution costs
    ~70 LOC and avoids redesigning the parser. Pruning the orphaned
    synthetic POIs from nodes.json (not just rewriting edges) prevents the
    map from rendering ghost POIs at jittered anchor coords.
  - **↔-RHS extraction is necessary even when pre-paren strip succeeds.**
    The Doha file authors cross-border destinations as
    `"Doha ↔ Manama (Bahrain)"`. Pre-paren strip → "Doha ↔ Manama" still
    contains both endpoints; need explicit ↔ split + RHS-as-candidate to
    resolve. This doubled rewrites from 40 → 80.
  - **Stub-count is the right shipping metric, not anchor_node_id NULL%.**
    `anchor_node_id` is binary present/absent. Stubs are the user-visible
    rendering defect. Use: declared distance > 50 nm AND geometric distance
    < 20% of declared (in nm-equivalent degrees). 153 → 103 in this pass.

source-discovered:
  - HK 0-outbound is NOT a pipeline bug — `hong-kong.md` has no
    `## Sub-clusters` table at all (its Players + Constraints + Waterways
    sections are dense but the schema's edge-source convention requires
    sub-cluster rows OR resolvable prose-↔ mentions). Recommend authoring
    HK sub-clusters (Discovery Bay, Sai Kung, Aberdeen, Repulse Bay,
    Outlying Islands, Macau corridor, HKIA SkyPier). v8 audit conflated
    "no sub-clusters" with "pipeline broken for HK".
  - RSG anchor `[36.85, 26.0]` with `anchor_name: "RSG/AMAALA maritime
    midpoint (Lagoon)"` is offshore (lon 36.85 is ~10 km west of the KSA
    Red Sea coast at lat 26°N). The v8 audit's "~35 nm inland" claim does
    not match current `city-anchors.json`. Either the audit pre-dates a
    v7 follow-up that set the maritime-midpoint, OR the audit was reading
    a SUB-CLUSTER row (RSG-internal Shura/Sheybarah/Triple Bay coords)
    rather than the anchor. Recommend visual re-check before any anchor
    move.
  - `locale-osm-cache.json` and `osm-cache.json` operate on POI IDs that
    INCLUDE the slug variant — so after pruning synthetic POIs, the OSM
    caches contain dead entries for IDs like
    `doha-qatar__doha-manama-bahrain`. Harmless (apply_coords lookup just
    misses) but worth a one-time cache-prune sweep when next geocoding.
  - There's locale-vs-POI duplication: same source row produces both a
    POI (slug strips "/") and a locale (slug preserves "/"), so e.g.
    "Ras Al Hadd / Ras Al Jinz" generates `ras-al-hadd-ras-al-jinz` AND
    `ras-al-hadd-/-ras-al-jinz`. Out of scope for this surgical fix but
    worth unifying the two slugify functions next sweep.

routes-flipped:
  80 edges flipped from "synthetic POI / jittered" to "real distant
  destination". Includes 22 MENA cross-border radials (Muscat↔Dubai,
  Doha↔Manama, etc.), 10+ Indonesian inter-arc, 5 Thai/Malaysian gulf-
  strait. Equivalent to the v8 audit's 28 visual stubs + their adjacency.

org-aliases:
  Three alias-cluster gaps from the dossier-enrichment pipeline learning
  closed in `canonicalize_orgs.py`:
  - Marriott rule: added `\bmarriott luxury (group|collection)\b` +
    `\britz[- ]carlton\b` (was leaking to "other")
  - LVMH rule: added `\bbulgari (hotel|resort)\b` aliases AND removed
    `bulgari hotel` from the Marriott rule (Bulgari brand is LVMH-owned;
    the Marriott Bulgari JV is operationally-true but brand-canonically
    misleading — pick brand-owner for canonical attribution)
  - Yandex rule: added `\byango\b` (Yango is Yandex's international ride-
    hail brand)

efficiency:
  - Build run time unchanged (~30s); the cross-cluster pass is O(n_pois ×
    avg-resolver-cost) ≈ 380 × O(1 hash lookup × ~5 candidates) ≈ ms.
  - Pruned-POI count (90 of 427 = 21%) is meaningful payload reduction
    for the front-end nodes.json fetch (743 KB → 437 KB).

suggested-update:
  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/SCHEMA.md
  change: |
    Document `cross_cluster_resolved: bool` and `cross_cluster_match_type`
    on edges. Note the synthetic-POI-pruning step in the build pipeline
    overview so future agents don't re-introduce them by reading parse-
    stage output as ground truth.

  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/parse_city_files.py
  change: |
    Long-term: classify cross-cluster sub-cluster rows AT PARSE time
    (heuristic: name contains "cross-border" / "↔" / "(via " / parenthetical
    country code) and emit them as edge-stubs with `to_node_id_raw` instead
    of synthetic POIs. Would let resolve_cross_file_edges.py only do the
    name-resolution and not the prune step, simplifying the data model.
    Lower priority — current downstream fix is correct and idempotent.

  target-file: /agent/home/navier/world-map/regions/east-asia/hong-kong.md
  change: |
    Add a `## Sub-clusters` table (HK Island / Kowloon anchor, Discovery
    Bay, Lamma, Cheung Chau, Peng Chau / Mui Wo, Sai Kung, Aberdeen /
    Repulse Bay / Stanley, HKIA SkyPier, Macau corridor, optionally
    Shenzhen / Zhuhai as OUT-OF-SCOPE mainland). Without this, HK remains
    0-outbound on the atlas regardless of any pipeline state. Treat as a
    content-authoring back-fill, not a pipeline fix.

### 2026-05-28 — country-shell split batch stub generation (19 sub-cities, 7 shells)
subagent: navier-city-stub.md (batch override mode)
task: Carve 19 sub-city stubs out of Japan/Korea/Taiwan/Turkey/Cambodia/Malaysia/Vietnam country shells per country-shell-split-plan.md
learnings:
  - methodology: **Batch-19 thin-stub generation via single Python template is the right shape** when (a) parent provides a defended-wedge plan doc with pre-extracted fields per child, (b) all 19 share one template structure, and (c) full dossier pass is explicitly deferred. Avoided rediscovering archetype / posture / wedge / named operators per node — pulled directly from plan-doc extract. ~5 minutes total runtime for 19 files vs. ~30 minutes if each were a full dossier-class file.
  - methodology: **Thin-stub vs. full-retrofit-stub is a distinct mode worth schema-naming.** Existing `navier-city-stub.md` spec implies full schema fill; batch-override payload here explicitly authorized `[stub — needs dossier pass]` placeholders on ~80% of sections. This is the right mode for split-wave fan-out where downstream dossier subagents will do the heavy lift. Suggest schema-naming this as "thin-stub mode" with the convention: snapshot + anchor + archetypes + named wedge + named operators filled; all other sections placeholdered with `[stub — needs dossier pass]` + cross-link to parent country shell.
  - schema-gap: **`parent_country_shell` + `is_country_shell: true` + `child_cities: [...]` lineage fields are NOT in CITY_TEMPLATE.md.** Added them ad-hoc in the front-matter banner + a country-shell banner block. Should be promoted to schema as a top-level metadata block (similar to `_Region:` / `_Posture:` / `_Last updated:`). Once the data spine consumes them, atlas-side queries like "all children of `vietnam` shell" become trivial.
  - schema-gap: **Country-shell-vs-sub-city role distinction has no canonical representation.** The 7 shells now play a different role from city-files (national-protocol envelope, FY/blackout/FX/regulator canonical at shell level; sub-cluster wedges migrate to children). Schema should add an explicit `role: country-shell | sub-city | hub-radial-anchor | standalone-cluster` field rather than implying it from filename pattern.
  - source-discovered: Turkey country shell lives at `regions/europe/turkey.md`, NOT under `mena/`. Cross-region migration history. Worth flagging in any country→region map (data spine builders should consult `regions/*/` glob, not assume region-from-country lookup).
  - efficiency: For batch-N stub generation, a single Python script with an inline LIST of (region, fname, parent_shell, title, posture, anchor, archetypes, snapshot, named_wedge, competitors, operators) tuples is materially faster than N parallel `write_file` calls. Reusable pattern for future split-waves.
suggested-update:
  target-file: /agent/home/navier/world-map/_schema/CITY_TEMPLATE.md
  change: |
    Add v2026-05-28 addendum section VV "Country-shell vs. sub-city role distinction + lineage fields":
      - Front-matter MUST include `parent_country_shell: {shell-id}` for sub-city files
      - Front-matter MUST include `is_country_shell: true` + `child_cities: [list]` for country-shell files
      - Add `role:` field (country-shell | sub-city | hub-radial-anchor | standalone-cluster | sister-pair-member | sister-trio-member)
      - Document thin-stub mode: snapshot + anchor + archetypes + named wedge + named operators filled; other sections may carry `[stub — needs dossier pass]` markers pending downstream dossier-class retrofit.
  target-file: /agent/home/navier/subagents/navier-city-stub.md
  change: |
    Add explicit "thin-stub mode" section documenting batch-split-wave usage:
      - Triggered when parent payload says "thin stub" / "needs dossier pass" / "batch override"
      - Pre-fill: anchor coord, posture, archetype, named wedge, primary competitors, named operators
      - Mark all other schema sections with `[stub — needs dossier pass]`
      - Add lineage fields (parent_country_shell, etc.)
      - Single-Python-script batch generation is the recommended pattern for >5 stubs in one wave

### 2026-05-28 — v9 atlas deploy + audit (full pipeline + post-deploy visual)
subagent: navier-atlas-visual-audit.md (override mode — deploy then audit)
task: Build data-spine v9 → copy outputs to atlas-external → run build.py → vercel prod deploy → audit 15 boarding-pt cities + 19 split stubs
learnings:
  - **POI icon-type-collapse check (Audit protocol §7) was producing false-positive in v7/v8 audits.** The mandatory check `new Set(features.map(f=>f.properties.type)).size === 1` ALWAYS fires on this codebase because `type: "poi"` is the geojson-feature-class marker (POI vs city vs locale), not the glyph-type. The actual glyph differentiator is `bp_type` (boarding-point injected, 17 distinct values across 782 features) or `poi_class` (parse-derived, 12 distinct values). Revise audit protocol §7 to check `bp_type` and `poi_class` instead. v9 glyph rewrite is actually working — the v8 P0 "all circles" finding was likely a real bug at that time but the §7 check would still false-positive even with working glyphs.
  - **New stub class introduced by 19-split-wave + country-shell authoring.** Sub-cluster destination POIs in newly-authored standalone-cluster and country-shell files have `coords_resolved: false` + `coords_source: "fallback-jitter-around-parent"`. They render as stub edges (declared 200-400nm collapse to <1nm geom). v9's `resolve_cross_file_edges.py` destination resolver fixes *cross-file* references but NOT *intra-file* sub-cluster rows missing OSM hits. Net stub count went UP 28→58 because new content introduced stubs faster than v9 closed cross-border ones. **Stub count by itself is a misleading shipping metric across waves where new content is added** — should report as "stubs by class" (cross-border-resolved vs intra-file-unresolved) so progress on one class isn't masked by regression on another.
  - **Per-anchor anchor-name read-out is critical for RSG-class anchor audits.** v9 RSG anchor at [36.85, 26.0] = "RSG/AMAALA maritime midpoint (Lagoon)" — visually offshore confirmed. Pre-flight reading of city-anchors.json before judging "inland" is the right discipline (matches v9-prebuild learning).
  - **Hong Kong renders fine at v9 even with 0 outbound edges.** The injected boarding-points (`hong-kong-boarding-points.json`) provide 12 visible POI glyphs (Discovery Bay, Sai Kung, Aberdeen, etc.). Visual completeness ≠ data-model completeness — outbound routes still 0 pending content back-fill of `## Sub-clusters` table. Worth flagging at audit time that the boarding-points injection is masking the underlying data gap.
  - **Dossier enrichment spot-check methodology works.** Filter humans by org-name substring (atlantis/capella) + filter by associated-cities containing slug → check `dossier_context` boolean + `dossier_posture` enum. Found 153/648 humans + 106/1213 orgs enriched. Should add this as Audit protocol §12 (dossier enrichment spot-check) since it's a recurring v9+ check.
  - **`destinationPath` on browser evaluate can hit "EIO: i/o error" when writing to /agent/home/...** if the destination directory doesn't exist OR if the path can't be opened atomically. Workaround: pre-create the file with `touch` via run_command, or fall back to inline JSON.stringify return. Note: inline returns are size-limited.
  - **Camera deep-link `?city=X` only fires fly-to on initial page load, not on subsequent same-page navigations to same `?city=X#fragment` URLs.** Workaround: drive via `evaluate: window.map.jumpTo(...)` instead of `navigate: ?city=...`. URL hash-fragment then updates via the map's own moveend listener. This matches the v7 audit's "one-way deep-link" finding from a different angle.
  - **3-screenshot batches with jumpTo+wait_for+take_screenshot are the right shape** for multi-city audits. 12 cities in 4 browser calls + filesystem cp from /agent/blocks took ~3 minutes vs. estimated ~12 minutes with one-shot-per-call. Reusable pattern for future audit fan-out.
  - **Build identity stamp discipline (§13)** worked: meta.json `generated_at: 2026-05-28T22:39:05.204306Z` captured. Cited in v9-SUMMARY.md.
suggested-update:
  target-file: /agent/subagents/navier-atlas-visual-audit.md (or /agent/home/navier/subagents/...)
  change: |
    Revise Audit protocol §7 POI icon-type-collapse check: query `bp_type` and `poi_class` distinct-value count, NOT `type`. Threshold: ≥10 distinct values across pop of >500 POIs is the "differentiated" pass; <5 is collapse P0.
    Add Audit protocol §12: dossier enrichment spot-check — fetch humans.json + orgs.json, filter by canonical-org-name + city-slug, assert ≥1 enriched (dossier_context=True or dossier_notes non-empty) on test orgs (Dubai Atlantis, Singapore Capella, RSG).
    Add Audit protocol §13: report stub-count by CLASS not in aggregate — (a) cross-border-resolved, (b) intra-file-jitter-collapsed (coords_resolved:false), (c) content-gap (no sub-cluster authored). Progress on one class shouldn't mask regression on another.
    Add Audit protocol §14: drive city navigation via `evaluate: window.map.jumpTo()` not `navigate: ?city=...` to bypass the one-way deep-link behavior.
  target-file: /agent/home/navier/app/data-spine/parse_city_files.py (or new geocode-sub-clusters.py step)
  change: |
    Add an intra-file sub-cluster OSM geocoding pass for POIs where `coords_resolved:false` and `distance_nm_from_anchor > 5`. Reuse osm-cache.json. Either query Nominatim with the POI `name` or prefer a curated `world-map/_data/place-coords.json` gazetteer. Expected impact: stub count 58 → ~10 across the 19 split-wave + 7 country-shell files. Without this, every future wave of standalone-cluster / country-shell authoring will reintroduce stubs.

### 2026-05-28 — HK sub-clusters back-fill + Oman coastal POI overrides (surgical content)
subagent: navier-city-retrofit.md (OVERRIDE — content authoring, not retrofit)
task: Two surgical content fixes — (1) author missing `## Sub-clusters` table in hong-kong.md (root cause of HK 0-outbound edges per v9-prebuild + v9 deploy audits); (2) add Sur/Ras Al Hadd/Khasab to manual poi-overrides.json (residual Oman stubs per v9-prebuild audit).
learnings:
  - **HK 0-outbound was definitively a content gap, not a pipeline bug — confirmed by file inspection.** The pre-existing prose explicitly said "Single-node city (HKSAR is one administrative + regulatory unit); no Sub-clusters table." This authoring choice predates schema v2026-05-29 addendum's framing that single-regulatory-unit ≠ single-operational-node. **Single-administrative-unit / multi-operational-node is a distinct cluster archetype** (HKSAR, Singapore arguably, future Bahrain/Manama) that REQUIRES a Sub-clusters table even though one regulator owns everything. Worth promoting to CITY_TEMPLATE addendum.
  - **Macau corridor is the headline Pioneer-out → Quanta-LR flip for HK.** Pre-existing prose framed HK↔Macau as "edge-of-N30 with effectively zero margin; feasible only with confirmed Macau-side pedestal" — i.e., gated by a slow cross-border Macau Maritime Authority concession ask. Under Quanta-LR diesel-refuel the pedestal-concession gate falls away entirely; Macau Outer Harbour + Taipa become a routine H2 2026+ wedge. This is the same pattern as MENA's AUH↔Doha/Manama/Khasab flip — Quanta-LR collapses cross-jurisdiction pedestal-concession asks across the board.
  - **Shenzhen/Zhuhai sub-cluster rows should be tagged "OUT OF SCOPE — strategic hold (NOT range)" with `out-of-corpus` jurisdiction**, distinct from "OUT OF SCOPE — pitch trap" (range-gated) or schema-canonical role taxonomy. The constraint is KB §8 mainland-China policy posture, not pilot geography or platform range. Both Pioneer II AND Quanta-LR are technically in-range. **Schema gap**: role taxonomy currently has no canonical row for "strategic-hold cross-border out-of-corpus." Used `OUT OF SCOPE — strategic hold` ad-hoc; recommend codifying.
  - **Kai Tak Cruise Terminal + Sports Park event pontoon = single open charging-spec window in HK** (per v9-prebuild coastal-POI addendum, now elevated to Sub-clusters row). Every other HK pier is either TD-managed legacy (closed) or club-microgrid retrofittable (open at marina level, but slow). Kai Tak is greenfield + dual-anchor open-spec + ESG/heritage co-brand surface — the single highest-leverage pedestal design-in target city-wide.
  - **Slugifier slash variants confirmed live in nodes.json.** `muscat-oman__ras-al-hadd-ras-al-jinz-turtle-reserve` AND `muscat-oman__ras-al-hadd-/-ras-al-jinz-turtle-reserve` both present; same for Khasab. Added BOTH variants as overrides keyed to same coord. Pass #11 PROMOTION NOTE deferred-list flagged "locale-vs-POI slugify unification" — this confirms the duplication is still live and is now blocking residual-stub resolution unless overrides cover both variants. **Until unified, every manual override for sub-clusters with `/` in the source name must add both slug variants.** Worth promoting to manual-coords AUTHORING NOTE.
  - **HK file has `## Operating Constraints` BEFORE `## Waterways & Routes`** — inverted from CITY_TEMPLATE order (Snapshot → Sub-clusters → Waterways → Constraints). Did not re-order in this surgical pass (out of scope); flagging as a future light-retrofit candidate.
routes-flipped:
  - **HK↔Macau (Outer Harbour + Taipa)** flipped from edge-of-Pioneer + Macau-pedestal-gated → Quanta-LR routine wedge (H2 2026+). Single most valuable HK retrofit-class flip, equivalent in pattern to AUH↔Doha/Manama/Khasab. Diesel-refuel removes the cross-border Macau Maritime & Water Authority pedestal-concession ask. Cross-border SAR sensitivity (TurboJET/Cotai Water Jet incumbents) remains the human-side gate; technical/range gate falls away.
  - HK↔Shenzhen + HK↔Zhuhai are NOT flipped (both already Pioneer-in-range); kept as OUT OF SCOPE — strategic hold (NOT range) per KB §8 mainland-China policy posture. Listed for graceful-redirect of partner-pitched routes.
  - 12 intra-HK legs (Central↔TST, Wan Chai, DB, Lamma, Cheung Chau, Peng Chau, Mui Wo, Sai Kung, Aberdeen, Repulse Bay, Kai Tak, SkyPier) all Pioneer-in-range — no flips, but now newly enumerated as sub-cluster rows so they emit outbound edges in the next data-spine build.
schema-gaps:
  - Role taxonomy missing canonical row for "OUT OF SCOPE — strategic hold (cross-border out-of-corpus, NOT range)" — used for Shenzhen/Zhuhai. Distinct from "OUT OF SCOPE — pitch trap" (range-gated graceful redirect). Recommend adding to CITY_TEMPLATE Sub-clusters role taxonomy.
  - Counterparty-jurisdiction column has no canonical value for "out-of-corpus" mainland PRD (or other out-of-corpus jurisdictions). Used the literal string `out-of-corpus`; should be codified.
  - "Single-administrative-unit / multi-operational-node" as a cluster archetype is not named in the schema. HK, possibly Singapore, possibly future Bahrain/Manama files all fit it. Recommend adding to the Sub-clusters preamble guidance.
reference-files:
  - Did NOT touch `/agent/home/navier/reference/*.md` cross-links (out of scope for this surgical pass; full retrofit would).
  - Did NOT update `/agent/home/navier/reference/out-of-range-marquee-routes.md` with HK↔Macau Pioneer-out → Quanta-LR flip; flagging as follow-up.

### 2026-05-28 — coastal-poi bodrum
subagent: navier-coastal-poi.md
task: Curated 69 boarding points across Bodrum peninsula + Datça + Marmaris + Göcek/Fethiye multi-cluster arc + cross-Aegean TR↔GR pointers + factory-market Türk Loydu cert anchor.
learnings:
  - **Bodrum fits multi-cluster-arc archetype #4 (Bali/Phuket family)** with strong D-Marin (Doğuş) chain-MOU consolidation overlay. Counterparty-stack typology is 4-deep (D-Marin Doğuş + Milta Koç + Setur Koç + Global Ports Holding) with Türk Loydu cert authority + İçmeler gulet shipwrights as cultural-heritage tier. D-Marin chain-MOU spans Yalıkavak + Turgutreis + Didim + Göcek + Netsel Marmaris = 5 marinas under one agreement — single-MOU-unlocks-5-anchors is the headline Bodrum wedge.
  - **Cross-border TR↔GR enumeration**: Bodrum↔Kos (~10 nm) + Marmaris↔Rhodes (~30 nm) + Datça↔Symi (~15 nm) — 3 distinct TR↔GR ICQ-handshake pairs in single file. Authored as cross-border pointers per pass-#9 rule 1 (routine cross-border >10 nm). Bodrum↔Kos is catalogue candidate #11 globally; Marmaris↔Rhodes #12. **Factory-market Turkey adjacency makes these the highest-leverage first-of-class EU↔non-EU regulatory pairings** in the wedge catalogue.
  - **Gateway-concentration POI ABSENCE confirmed at BOTH Bodrum airports** (BJV Milas-Bodrum + DLM Dalaman serving Göcek/Fethiye) — extends pass-#9 universal-pattern to Turkey. Russian/EU charter-pax + Turkish-domestic demand mix; uncontested-concession-upside open.
  - **Public-water-mobility ABSENCE confirmed** despite Bodrum bay being canonical multi-locale-around-single-bay geography (Bodrum / Bitez / Gümbet / Türkbükü / Yalıkavak / Gündoğan) where intra-bay sea-shuttle SHOULD obviously exist but doesn't. Adds Turkey to the 10+ city ABSENCE-wedge list. 7th MENA/MED instance.
  - **NEW seasonal hide sub-variant: Meltemi operational overlay (Jul–Sep)** — Aegean afternoon N–NW gale routine 30–40 kts; not exclusion but afternoon-departure-discipline gate. Distinct from monsoon (no monsoon in Med), distinct from Khareef/cyclone seasonal hides. Propagation candidates: Greek Aegean (same Meltemi), Adriatic Bora, Mistral Côte d'Azur, NE-Lodos Mediterranean Turkey.
  - **Republican executive-residence hide-royal-overlook positive instance: Okluk Bay Cumhurbaşkanlığı Yazlık Külliyesi** (Turkish Presidential summer compound near Marmaris). Extends the republican-extension pattern (Malacañang canonical) to Turkey. August-residency demo-cadence avoidance + overflight scheduling protocol.
  - **Aksaz Naval Base** (TR Navy southern fleet) is single hide-military positive Marmaris-cluster; Bodrum peninsula proper has no military overlay (verified negative).
  - **Cultural-heritage shipwright tier extends to Bodrum İçmeler + Marmaris Bozburun gulet yards** — 4th instance (after Bira Bugis phinisi, Aswan felucca, Sur sambuk). Pan-Med gulet-shipwright community is cultural-channel partner not MRO-counterparty; pairs with Türk Loydu cert authority for factory-market Turkey adjacency narrative.
  - **Seasonal-complementarity-pair POI** between Bodrum (Aegean) ↔ Antalya (Med) — Turkey-internal analog of Samui↔Phuket / Komodo↔RA. Authored as P2 complementarity-pair entry. D-Marin chain-MOU spans both ends.
  - **Brand-gap-as-wedge applied to Aman Bodrum pipeline** entry — pairs with land-only Amanruya, NEVER author land-only Aman without Sampriti HQ alignment first; pipeline = greenfield class-1 charging-window design-in target.
  - **Hotel_jetty share 17% (12/69)** — Bodrum sits BELOW hospitality-anchor benchmark (~30–50%) despite dense luxury hotels, because marina + hospitality coexist as separate boarding modes (D-Marin marinas serve charter; hotels also have private jetties). Closer to intermediate-scheduled-network archetype #3 by mix despite multi-cluster-arc geography. New mixed archetype.

Distinct from cross-cluster Samui↔Phuket / Komodo↔RA pairs — this is sub-cluster ↔ sub-cluster within ONE island, requiring 3-developer coordinated pier-flip discipline. Propagation candidates: Boracay habagat (Tambisaan↔White Beach already partially-recognized), Bali Sanur↔Kuta, Koh Lanta E↔W.
  - **Cable-car maritime arrival pier as intermodal showcase** — Hon Thom Sun World cable car (~7.9 km, longest single-span over-sea cable car globally per Guinness) creates a sea↔air↔cable handoff at An Thoi station. First atlas instance of `cable-car-to-boat handoff` type narrative — Navier-experience showcase under Sun Group single-MOU. Propagation candidates: future Langkawi SkyCab maritime tie-in, Vinpearl Nha Trang cable car (sea-crossing, similar pattern).
  - **VN↔KH cross-border catalogue-#7 anchor confirmed**: Phu Quoc↔Sihanoukville (~70 nm, edge-of-Pioneer-II / firm Quanta-LR) + Phu Quoc↔Koh Rong (~60 nm Pioneer II viable) + Phu Quoc↔Kep (~30 nm shortest). Three distinct VN↔KH ICQ-handshake pairs in single file. First Vietnam-side cross-border-pointer cluster — Brevié Line historical maritime boundary authored as hide entry for ICQ-discipline framing (NEGATIVE for active-conflict, POSITIVE for cross-jurisdiction-pedestal-concession overhead). Sun Group An Thoi marina pipeline + Royal Group Cambodia (Koh Rong) + Six Senses Krabey Island = 3-counterparty cross-border stack.
  - **GATEWAY-CONCENTRATION POI ABSENCE confirmed at PQC** (Phu Quoc Int'l Airport, ~5M pax 2023) — no incumbent airport↔resort marine handoff; Sun Air seaplane (Twin Otters under Sun Group) provides closest analog → highest-leverage uncontested concession-upside given Sun Group single-counterparty consolidation. Adds Vietnam to 16+ ABSENCE-instance list; flavor = single-developer-microgrid-bundled (distinct from Hajj / Russian-charter / event-circuit / sovereign-summit / conservation flavors).
  - **PUBLIC-WATER-MOBILITY ABSENCE confirmed** despite coastal-arc geography ideal for it (Dương Đông ↔ Hàm Ninh ↔ Bãi Trường ↔ An Thới natural coastal hop). Adds Vietnam to SEA-side ABSENCE list (Bali/Jakarta/Lombok/LBJ/RA → now PQ = 6 SEA instances).
  - **Phu Quoc archetype calibration**: P0 share 24% (12/49); hotel_jetty share 35% (17/49); ferry_terminal share 29% (14/49). Sits BETWEEN hospitality-anchor archetype #2 (AUH ~30% hotel_jetty) and closed-destination single-counterparty archetype #5 (RSG ~56% hotel_jetty) — closer to hybrid #2 due to 3-developer consolidation rather than 1-counterparty. New mixed archetype candidate: "multi-counterparty private-developer-portfolio hospitality island" (3-developer-consolidation in single SEZ).
  - **Phu Quoc SEZ** (Special Economic Zone, recently elevated to Phu Quoc City status 2021) = regulatory sandbox surface — Pioneer II MASS pilot wedge alongside Sun Group + Vingroup + BIM developer counterparties. Distinct from Komodo/RA UNESCO-overlay sandboxes; closer to RSG SEZ / NEOM regulatory frame at smaller scale.
  - **hide-protected COMPOSITE entry**: Phu Quoc National Park (314 km², ~50% of island) + UNESCO Kien Giang Biosphere Reserve (2006) + Ramsar wetland (N-coast Vũng Bầu seagrass dugong habitat) = single COMPOSITE hide entry (per pass-#10 sub-rule). Distinct from authoring 3 separate adjacent ecological hides.
  - **DUAL-MONSOON pier-flip discipline** = NE-monsoon Nov–Mar (E-coast Bai Vong + Ham Ninh exposed) + SW-monsoon May–Oct (W-coast Bai Truong + Bai Dai + Ong Lang exposed). Sun Group Khem Beach S-cluster = year-round shelter. Authored as hide-water-body fishing-fleet-baseline-with-monsoon-flush sub-variant 6.
  - **Sun Group An Thoi Marina pipeline = single highest-leverage class-1 OPEN-SPEC charging-window on Phu Quoc** — pipeline asset under permitting; first-mover pedestal-design-in window. Single Sun MOU unlocks: (a) An Thoi marina pipeline, (b) JW Marriott + New World + Premier Village + Sailing Club + Sun Premier Hon Thom hotel jetties (5 properties), (c) Hon Thom cable car maritime arrival pier, (d) Sun Air seaplane PQC handoff. Possibly densest single-counterparty unlock in SEA atlas.
  - **Aman chain-gap-as-wedge anti-author entry** added — Aman has no Phu Quoc property; Quanta-LR = Aman quiet-mode answer; never author land-Aman without Sampriti HQ alignment. Third Aman chain-gap anti-author after Komodo + RA.

### 2026-05-28 — coastal-poi ha-long-bay
subagent: navier-coastal-poi.md
task: Curated 45 boarding points across Ha Long / Bãi Cháy / Hòn Gai + Tuần Châu + Lan Ha / Cát Bà + Bái Tử Long / Vân Đồn + Hai Phong feeder + cross-border VN↔CN Móng Cái pointer.
learnings:
  - **FOUR-developer single-portfolio cluster** (Sun Group + Vingroup + BIM Group + Tuan Chau Group + Vinaconex ITC pipeline = arguably 5) — denser convener stack than Phu Quoc (3-developer) on the Quảng Ninh side. Sun Group is single-largest unlock (International Cruise Port + Sun World Complex + Ba Deo Cable Car + Sun Premier Village + Vân Đồn Airport + Hai Au-adjacent seaplane = ~6-asset MOU bundle). New mixed-archetype candidate: "multi-counterparty private-developer hospitality cluster overlaid on UNESCO-protected core" — Phu Quoc was 3-developer-SEZ; Ha Long is 4–5-developer-UNESCO.
  - **UNESCO-2023 Cat Ba contiguous extension makes Ha Long the first SEA atlas instance of DUAL-CONTIGUOUS UNESCO sites** (Ha Long 1994 + Cat Ba 2023 archipelago — first-ever transboundary-style intra-Vietnam UNESCO contiguous expansion). Composite hide-protected entry covers both. Foiling-permit gate routes through BQL Vịnh Hạ Long (Quảng Ninh-side) + Cat Ba MD (Hai Phong-side) — DUAL provincial regulator coordination is itself a structural overhead worth flagging in the city file.
  - **Tuan Chau Group as private-island junk-fleet landlord-of-record** — single private-counterparty (Đào Hồng Tuyển family-office) controls ~80% of UNESCO-core junk-fleet departures via Tuan Chau Marina + Cruise Wharf pontoons. Distinct from Sun Group cruise terminal (international cruise) and Vingroup Reu (island-resort). 4th private-island-as-marina-landlord pattern after Vinpearl Reu / Tuan Chau / Phu Quoc-Sun-Group / Sentosa-SDC. Pioneer II quiet-mode incumbent-upgrade conversation runs entirely through Tuan Chau Group not Vinamarine.
  - **Hai Phong cross-province feeder is essential cluster scope** — Got/Cai Vieng RoRo + Bến Bính passenger wharf + Hai Phong river-debris hide + Damen Song Cam shipyard partner + Hai Phong Naval Region 1 hide cluster. Cross-province enumeration discipline matches Sharjah east-coast / Jakarta-SG-Batam cross-border pattern (administering-province enumerates contiguous-province POIs for operational routing). Effectively Ha Long city file scope = Quảng Ninh + Hai Phong dual-province for boarding.
  - **GATEWAY-CONCENTRATION POI ABSENCE confirmed at VDO Vân Đồn** — Vietnam's first privately-developed (Sun Group) international airport; no incumbent airport↔marine handoff to Cai Rong / Bai Tu Long resorts (~5 nm). Single-developer-microgrid-bundled flavor (parallel to Phu Quoc PQC Sun Group lane). Adds VDO as 2nd VN instance after PQC.
  - **PUBLIC-WATER-MOBILITY ABSENCE confirmed** at Bãi Cháy↔Hòn Gai intra-bay (~3 nm Cua Luc crossing) — Bãi Cháy Bridge (2006) explicitly eliminated the ferry. Canonical absence-wedge geography. Adds VN to 7 SEA instances.
  - **NE-monsoon + W-Pacific-typhoon DUAL operational overlay** — distinct from Samui Gulf single-monsoon and Phu Quoc dual-monsoon-internal-flip patterns. Tonkin Gulf is typhoon-recurve corridor (Tembin 2012, Sơn Tinh 2018, Talim 2023 direct hits) layered on top of NE-monsoon Nov–Mar + Jan–Feb cold-surge fog visibility constraint. 3rd DUAL-overlay instance after Salalah (Khareef + Arabian Sea cyclone) and Lombok (strait-gate + monsoon).
  - **VN↔CN strategic-hold cross-border pointer** authored at Móng Cái / Vạn Gia — Beibu Gulf maritime boundary settled 2000 (NEGATIVE for active conflict) but OUT OF SCOPE strategic-hold cross-border out-of-corpus per KB §8 mainland-China policy posture. Parallel to HK↔Shenzhen/Zhuhai posture. First VN-side instance of the role-taxonomy gap flagged in pass-#11 HK retrofit (still unresolved schema-side).
  - **3-tier shipyard-partner topology confirmed VN-side** — Halong Shipbuilding/SBIC (sovereign-naval tier 1) + Damen Song Cam JV (composite-commercial tier 2) + (cultural-heritage tier 3 absent in N-Vietnam; the wooden-junk shipwright tradition that built original Halong junks is largely industrialized now into Bhaya/Paradise fiberglass + steel fleet — gap from Bira/Phinisi / Aswan-felucca / Bodrum-İçmeler patterns). N-VN has 2-tier shipyard topology (no cultural-heritage tier).
  - **Sun Group VDO airport + International Cruise Port + Sun World Complex + Ba Deo Cable Car + Sun Premier Village = ~6-asset single-MOU bundle**, the densest single-counterparty unlock on Quảng Ninh side, mirroring Sun Group's Phu Quoc An Thoi 10-asset stack. Sun Group is the Vietnam-wide convener with cross-cluster single-MOU leverage (Da Nang Ba Na + Sapa + Phu Quoc + Ha Long + Cat Ba pipeline) — single most strategic VN counterparty after Vingroup.
  - **Hotel_jetty share 16% (7/45)**; ferry_terminal share 22%; public_pier share 27%; hide share 18%. Archetype mix sits closer to intermediate-scheduled-network archetype #3 than to hospitality-anchor #2, because the UNESCO public-pier + junk-fleet anchorage layer dominates over private resort jetties. New mixed candidate: "UNESCO-protected-core multi-developer-hospitality cluster" — distinct from Komodo (UNESCO + state-developer KEK + phinisi-channel) by having denser private-developer convener density and no state-developer envelope.
  - **Aman chain-gap-as-wedge entry** added — 4th instance after Komodo + RA + Phu Quoc; Amanoi Vinh Hy (SC Vietnam) is closest land property → never author Ha Long land-Aman without Sampriti HQ alignment first.

### 2026-05-28 — coastal-poi setouchi
subagent: navier-coastal-poi.md
task: Curated 66 boarding points across Setouchi (Seto Inland Sea) — Benesse Naoshima/Teshima/Inujima art-island axis + Shodoshima multi-port + Onomichi/Mihara/Setoda Shimanami Kaido + Imabari Shikoku + Hiroshima/Miyajima UNESCO + Kobe/Takamatsu line-haul + Triennale 2025/2028 design-in window.
learnings:
  - **NEW archetype candidate: "multi-cluster archipelago + dense-incumbent-ferry-network + single-counterparty-art-portfolio-anchor + Triennale-time-bound-window QUADRUPLE-OVERLAY"** — Setouchi sits between archetype #3 (intermediate scheduled-network) + #4 (multi-cluster-arc) + #9 (single state/portfolio anchor). Volume 66 (P0 27%, hotel_jetty 14%, ferry_terminal 53%); hotel_jetty share LOWER than hospitality-anchor archetypes because dense-ferry-network dominates POI mix. Benesse + Fukutake Foundation + Triennale Exec Cmte = single-portfolio-anchor (parallel to Lombok ITDC Mandalika) but consumer-cultural rather than state-developer-SEZ. Distinct enough to flag as archetype #12 candidate: "multi-cluster art-archipelago + dense-scheduled-ferry + cultural-foundation-portfolio-anchor."
  - **Setouchi Triennale 2028 = single highest-leverage Japan-wide TIME-BOUND design-in window.** Triennale runs Spring + Summer + Autumn 2025 + 2028 (Soichiro Fukutake + Fram Kitagawa). Event_pontoon class-4 open-spec authoring + Triennale Exec Cmte single-MOU spans 12+ art islands across Kagawa + Okayama. Pre-2028 pedestal-design-in window is now-or-never (time-decay framing — closes ~2027 for 2028 cycle, then 2030 for 2031 cycle). Add to charging-design-in cluster as JP-instance #1.
  - **PUBLIC-WATER-MOBILITY INCUMBENT-DENSE counterpart to pass-#9 ABSENCE wedge.** Setouchi is the OPPOSITE of Bali/Doha/AUH — 12+ named ferry operators (JR-W Miyajima + Setonaikai Kisen + Ishizaki Kisen + Shikoku Kisen + Jumbo Ferry + Shodoshima Ferry + Kokusai Ferry + Ryobi Ferry + Aqua Net + Meon + Mihara-Setoda + Marugame-Honjima) running ~100+ daily routes across 7 prefectures. Hybrid scheduled-network authoring per pass-#10 (per-pier endpoints + Sea Spica 8-stop network summary + Jumbo Ferry network summary + overall multi-operator summary). Pioneer II partner-not-compete framing essential.
  - **Sea Spica (JR-W/Setonaikai-Kisen high-design scheduled cruise)** = single-operator 8-stop network → summary-row enumeration per pass-#9 sub-rule (single-operator ≤30 stops). Distinct from Jumbo Ferry Kobe↔Takamatsu line-haul also single-operator. HK Central Piers per-pier-per-concession is the contrast pattern.
  - **Tsuneishi family channel = SINGLE-FAMILY CROSS-REGION bridge** between Setouchi hospitality (Bella Vista Spa + Guntu floating ryokan + Onomichi U2 + Discoverlink Setouchi) AND Tsuneishi Cebu PH-side shipyard (Manila/Cebu/Palawan coastal-POI shipyard-partner entry). Adds Japan-PH single-family cross-region inland-chain-anchor pattern alongside Sawiris EG↔OM Muriya (pass-#10) and Adrian-Zecha-Azumi-Setoda pan-Asia (pass-#10). Add as 3rd named instance of cross-country/region private-family counterparty leverage.
  - **Imabari Shipbuilding = JP shipyard-partner pointer.** World's largest shipbuilder by GT. Extends pass-#9/#10 shipyard-partner-pointer pattern beyond ID/QA/BH/UAE/PH to JP. Higaki family channel. Tsuneishi separately at Numakuma/Fukuyama = SECOND JP shipyard-partner. JP shipyard-partner ecosystem (Imabari Group + Tsuneishi + IHI + JMU + MHI Mihara) is structurally distinct counterparty-stack tier vs. SEA-tropical or MENA-arid contexts — multi-yard family-owned consolidation.
  - **Adrian Zecha Azumi Setoda cross-channel to Aman pan-Asia + Sherpa Capital Aman Setouchi pipeline + Janu by Aman pipeline.** Triple-pipeline Aman-group + post-Aman concentration in Setouchi — possibly highest single-region Aman/Zecha-channel density globally. Chain-gap-as-wedge anti-author discipline applies to Aman/Janu pipeline entries (Sampriti HQ alignment required pre-engagement). Azumi Setoda is the safe entry-via-Zecha back-channel.
  - **Hide-water-body NEGATIVE-confirmation entry: Setouchi structural calm-baseline** = canonical JP-side negative entry (parallel to Doha-Bay clean negative, Flores-Sea negative, Bahrain-Bay clean). Setouchi semi-enclosed inland sea + tidal-current-managed straits + foil-friendly low-debris baseline. Localised industrial-positive sub-variant at Mizushima Kombinat (JFE + Mitsubishi Chemical + Eneos = 4th largest JP petrochem complex). Operational-strait-gate sub-variant at Naruto whirlpool (~10 kts tidal current).
  - **Hide-royal-overlook NEGATIVE-confirmation entry: Imperial-family Setouchi absence.** Imperial-family villas (Hayama / Suzaki / Nasu) are NOT in Setouchi — structural ABSENCE of Imperial-protocol scheduling overhead anywhere on Inland Sea. Distinct from Tokyo Bay (Imperial Palace + Akasaka) and Sagami Bay (Hayama Imperial Villa). Structural-advantage marker.
  - **Hide-protected-ecological composite: Setonaikai National Park (Japan's first NP, 1934).** Covers ~75% of Setouchi shoreline. Per pass-#10 composite-overlay entry. Does NOT exclude operational passage but DOES gate any newbuild greenfield-shoreline pedestal civil-works (MoE permit-gate). Pioneer II pedestal-design-in via existing port/marina locations (already concession-grandfathered) bypasses the gate.
  - **Hide-military DUAL entry: JMSDF Kure (largest JMSDF base) + USMC Iwakuni (joint US/JP MCAS).** Setouchi west-end has BOTH foreign-base (USMC) AND domestic-base (JMSDF) overlays. Extends hide-military sub-vocabulary to foreign-base-on-host-nation-territory pattern (USMC Iwakuni / future US Yokosuka / Subic-PH historical / Diego Garcia). Distinct from single-nation overlays (Kure JMSDF / QENF Doha / Aksaz Marmaris / etc).
  - **Gateway-concentration POI ABSENCE confirmed at TAK + HIJ + OKJ.** All Setouchi airports inland; Kobe UKB Port-Island is only adjacent-coastal airport candidate. Light-magnitude variant; demand-driver = Triennale calendar-overlay surge (parallel to Lombok MotoGP). Adds JP to 16+ ABSENCE instance list.
  - **High-design Pioneer II pedestal showcase pair cluster** — Setouchi has densest concentration of starchitect-designed coastal hospitality globally: SANAA (Naoshima Marine Station) + Tadao Ando (Benesse House + Setouchi Aonagi) + Toyo Ito (Omishima Museum) + Shigeru Ban (Simose Art Museum + Villas) + Sou Fujimoto (Ogijima terminal + Naoshima New Museum 2025) + Ryue Nishizawa (Teshima Art Museum) + Yasushi Horibe (Guntu floating ryokan). 7 starchitect anchors in single multi-cluster file. Navier-Experience pedestal-design-in showcase showcase-of-showcases candidate. Add to authoring discipline: when high-design-architect density ≥5 in single file, surface as a distinct wedge surface.
  - **Seasonal-complementarity-pair: Setouchi (Apr-Oct calm + Triennale Spring/Summer/Autumn) ↔ Okinawa/Kerama (Nov-Mar winter-peak).** First JP-internal seasonal-complementarity-pair POI. Parallel to Samui↔Phuket / Komodo↔RA / Bodrum↔Antalya. Year-round JP Pioneer II fleet utilisation pair.
  - **Quanta-LR within-arc refuel-staging: Setouchi west↔east arc (Hiroshima↔Kobe ~200 nm) via Mihara/Fukuyama/Bizen-Hinase ENEOS bunkering.** Same-jurisdiction within-arc sub-variant (pass-#10 sub-rule). Sovereign-state-bunker (ENEOS/JX Nippon) primary-node sub-variant. Adds JP to intra-country mid-arc staging-point pattern.
  - **Cross-arc pointer: Sunflower Ferry Kobe↔Beppu (Kyushu) ~280 nm Quanta-LR-only.** Intra-country cross-arc pointer per pass-#9 rule 1. Mitsui OSK Sunflower line-haul incumbent. Adds JP-internal cross-arc pattern.

### 2026-05-28 — pipeline enhancement: intra-file sub-cluster OSM geocoding
subagent: navier-city-retrofit.md (override-mode pipeline task)
task: Added `geocode_sub_clusters.py` to data-spine build between parse + resolve_cross_file_edges, with `apply_coords.py` extended to consume `sub-cluster-osm-cache.json`. Targets the gap where v9 audit found 19 split-files + 7 country-shell files generated ~150 unresolved sub-cluster POIs collapsing to `fallback-jitter-around-parent`.
learnings:
  - **OSM Nominatim hit rate on intra-file sub-cluster POIs: 90.3% (234/259)** with `'{poi_name}, {parent_country}'` + parent-anchor bbox. **Unresolved POIs dropped 192 → 19** (90% reduction). This validates the build-pipeline approach over per-file manual overrides for the long tail.
  - **Country-shell anchors MUST disable the viewbox.** First-pass used 5° bbox around the country-anchor coord (Tokyo for Japan, KL for Malaysia, etc.). Result: `Setouchi, Japan` matched a Tokyo-area "Setouchi" within bbox; `Hokkaido coastal, Japan` matched a Tokyo-area string; `HCMC, Vietnam` matched a non-canonical district. Fix: country-shell anchors → no viewbox; rely on country-scoped text bias. City anchors → 3° bbox (1.5° was too tight for cross-border POIs like `dubai__khasab-musandam`).
  - **Parent-token suffix filter is essential for cross-cluster POIs.** Names like `"Doha ↔ Dammam (KSA Eastern Province)"` under parent `doha-qatar` would query `"Doha, Qatar"` first and return Doha. Heuristic: drop leading query tokens that match any parent-anchor slug token (>3 chars) so `Dammam` leads. Implemented; resolves ~10 cross-cluster pointers correctly.
  - **Headline stub-edge metric still shows only 58→54 because of LOCALE-vs-POI ID COLLISION (Pass #11 deferred slugifier-unification).** `apply_coords.py` strips prior locales then APPENDS new locale nodes; many locale ids collide with POI ids (e.g. `japan__okinawa-archipelago-...`). At runtime the last-write-wins overwrites POI sub-cluster coords with locale-fallback-jitter at parent anchor. When de-duplicating with POI-precedence the true stub-edge count drops further (75 raw → 54). **The slugifier-unification fix is now the highest-leverage single pipeline change available** — promote from deferred to immediate.
  - **Composite-route POI names should be excluded from intra-file edge generation.** Rows like `"Karimunjawa ↔ Bali direct"` or `"Manila↔Visayas trans-archipelago direct"` declare a ROUTE not a PLACE. OSM geocodes the leading endpoint and the edge becomes a stub (declared = route length, geom = ~0nm because both ends are the same city). Fix belongs in `parse_city_files.py`: skip emission when name contains `↔` AND no jurisdiction crossing OR when both tokens match the parent anchor.
  - **Meta-rows like `_OUT OF SCOPE — pitch trap_` should be filtered at parse-time.** Currently emit a POI + edge that always fails geocoding. `parse_city_files.py` should skip rows whose name starts with `_OUT OF SCOPE_` or whose role contains "OUT OF SCOPE" (≈5 zombie edges).
  - **OSM cache file separation works cleanly.** Keeping `osm-cache.json` (priority-story-city pass) and `sub-cluster-osm-cache.json` (this new pass) as two files lets each pass evolve independently. `apply_coords.py` reads them in priority order (overrides → osm → sub-cluster-osm → fallback-jitter). New `coords_source` tag `osm-nominatim-sub-cluster` lets downstream audits distinguish pass provenance.
  - **Re-running with cleared null entries successfully re-tries previously-failed queries** under relaxed viewbox rules. Authoring pattern for future pipeline iterations: invalidate-and-retry rather than rewrite cache from scratch. Saves 95% of Nominatim quota.
  - **25 POIs in the manual-gazetteer queue are mostly NOT geocodable.** 9 are meta-rows (`_OUT OF SCOPE_`), 5 are composite cross-border catalogues with no single referent, 5 are obscure named features OSM lacks (`Bandar Khairan`, `Hallaniyat Islands`, `Penghu Archipelago`, `Ang Thong Marine Park` non-canonical names). Only ~6 are likely candidates for genuine manual override.

### 2026-05-28 — coastal-poi antalya
subagent: navier-coastal-poi.md
task: Curated 49 boarding points across Antalya / Lycian Coast (Belek/Kemer/Olympos/Kekova/Kaş/Kalkan/Patara + Alanya/Side east-bay + AYT gateway + TR↔GR Kastellorizo cross-border + TRNC Girne cross-border pointer + Bodrum-pair seasonal-complementarity).
learnings:
  - **Antalya hotel_jetty share 24% (12/49), marina 33%, P0 share 22%** — sits closer to intermediate-archetype #3 than to Belek's mass-luxury-density signal would suggest, because (a) Belek hotels are soft-landing-only beach jetties not formal marinas; (b) D-Marin Kemer + Setur Antalya/Finike/Kaş trio + Alanya Marina + Kaleiçi public-municipal provide unusually strong marina infrastructure for a hospitality-dominated coast. Mixed archetype.
  - **D-Marin chain-MOU now spans Aegean (5 marinas) + Med (Kemer + Antalya pipeline) = 7-marina Doğuş single-MOU** — extends pass-#12 archetype #12 (Bodrum) cross-cluster. Antalya↔Bodrum seasonal-complementarity-pair (3rd globally after Samui↔Phuket + Komodo↔RA + Ha Long↔Phu Quoc + Setouchi↔Okinawa) anchored under SAME single-counterparty MOU — first instance of same-counterparty consolidating both ends of a seasonal-complementarity pair. Strategic-leverage call-out for TR convener stack.
  - **Setur Marinas (Koç-side) parallel-lane** Antalya + Finike + Kaş = 3-marina Antalya-arc trio under single Koç-Holding chain-MOU — distinct from Doğuş D-Marin on Aegean. Two-chain-duopoly TR marina counterparty stack (Doğuş D-Marin + Koç Setur) cleanly partitioned: D-Marin Aegean+Kemer / Setur Antalya-arc. Optionality-rich for cert-anchor pedestal-design-in.
  - **TR↔GR cross-border catalogue extends to #15 Kaş↔Kastellorizo (~1 nm)** = SHORTEST TR↔GR cross-border globally + 4th globally shortest cross-border-immigration-gated micro-corridor after Langkawi↔Koh Lipe MY↔TH + DCFT↔Tanah Merah MY↔SG + Kazungula.
